Method: 提出渐进式种子剪枝（PSP）：将计算预算前置，早期评估大量种子，对中间去噪估计进行评分，逐步缩小候选集，仅对最有希望的轨迹进行完整去噪，同时保持总模型评估次数固定。

Result: 在扩散和流匹配主干网络上，PSP在相同计算量下 consistently 优于 best-of-N、重要性采样和树搜索基线，在 GenEval 自动评分和人工评估上均取得更高 prompt-alignment 分数。

Conclusion: 放松内存占用固定的约束可以开辟新的推理时缩放维度，前置探索加激进剪枝的策略能更有效地利用固定计算预算。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching models dominate conditional image generation, yet inference-time scaling for these models is far less developed than for autoregressive language models. Because final quality is highly sensitive to the initial noise seed, many approaches spend extra compute on seed search or resampling under a black-box reward, but typically maintaining a constant memory footprint throughout inference. We show that relaxing this constraint enables an underexplored inference-time scaling axis: by front-loading exploration, evaluating many seeds early, and pruning aggressively, we can use a fixed compute budget more effectively. \emph{Progressive Seed Pruning} (\PSP) scores intermediate denoised estimates and progressively narrows the candidate set so that only promising trajectories are fully denoised, while keeping the total number of model evaluations fixed. Across diffusion and flow-matching backbones, \PSP \ consistently improves reward-guided selection and achieves higher GenEval scores (automated) and better human evaluation on prompt-alignment than best-of-$N$, importance-sampling, and tree-search baselines at matched compute. Project page: https://www.vision.caltech.edu/psp. Code: https://github.com/rogerioagjr/psp.

</details>


### [215] [Unified Video Dense Prediction from Disjoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21592)
*Yihong Sun,Seoung Wug Oh,Jiahui Huang,Bharath Hariharan,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniD是一个统一的视频模型，通过知识蒸馏从不相交的领域特定数据集中联合预测八种密集场景属性（几何、外观、语义），无需标注重叠或伪标签，利用预训练扩散模型的视觉先验弥合领域差距，在多个任务上达到与专精模型相当的性能，并展现出强大的跨任务一致性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景理解任务依赖于碎片化、领域不兼容的数据集，而当前的统一系统要么局限于完全共同标注的数据，要么因伪标签化而承受高计算成本。本文旨在解决如何从不相交的领域特定数据集中高效学习统一的多任务场景理解模型。

Method: 提出UniD模型，采用两阶段策略：首先在每个任务上训练专精教师网络，然后通过轻量级任务投影器将这些教师知识蒸馏到统一的骨干网络中。利用预训练扩散模型的强视觉先验来弥合不同训练数据源之间的领域差距，无需标注重叠或伪标签生成。

Result: UniD在八个密集预测任务上与专精任务模型和多任务基线相比具有竞争力的性能，在分布外场景中展现出强泛化能力，并增强了时间一致性和跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: 预训练扩散模型的视觉先验足以弥合不相交训练数据带来的领域差距，使统一模型能够泛化到训练中从未见过的场景-任务组合，为从碎片化数据中学习统一场景理解模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Scene understanding requires simultaneous prediction about geometry, appearance, and semantics. However, existing task-specific annotations are fragmented across incompatible, domain-specific datasets. Current unified systems circumvent this by restricting training to fully co-annotated data, or by incurring the large computational cost of pseudo-labeling. To mitigate this, we introduce UniD, a unified video model that jointly predicts eight dense scene properties-depth, surface normals, semantic segmentation, boundaries, human parts, albedo, shading, and materials-all learned from disjoint, domain-specific datasets. We propose a simple yet effective distillation step in which per-task experts supervise a unified backbone through lightweight task projectors, eliminating the need for annotation overlap or pseudo-labeling. Our key insight is that the strong visual priors of a pretrained diffusion model are sufficient to bridge the domain gaps introduced by disjoint training sources, enabling robust generalization to scene-task combinations never seen during training. UniD achieves competitive performance against per-task specialists and multi-task baselines, with strong generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and enhanced temporal and cross-task consistency. Code and video results are available at https://unid-video.github.io/.

</details>


### [216] [Streaming Multi-Agent Autoregressive Diffusion Model with World State Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21594)
*Sicheng Mo,Yuheng Li,Ziyang Leng,Krishna Kumar Singh,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeaver (W²) 是一种流式多智能体视频扩散模型，通过引入可学习的跨智能体世界状态寄存器来维护共享世界信息，并采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构分别建模世界状态和视觉帧，在双智能体Minecraft视频生成中提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅将观察历史作为条件上下文传递，在多智能体和多视角场景下难以维护共享的世界状态，导致逻辑不一致。

Method: 提出WorldWeaver (W²) 模型：(1) 引入跨智能体世界状态寄存器——可学习token，用于存储共享世界信息并跟踪个体智能体状态，在每段生成后动态更新；(2) 使用个体智能体状态、全局视图（如鸟瞰图）和场景文本作为监督信号；(3) 采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，用独立权重分别建模世界状态和视觉帧。

Result: 在双智能体Minecraft视频生成任务中，显式的世界状态建模显著提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。

Conclusion: 明确建模世界状态是提升多智能体视频生成逻辑一致性的有效方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent interactive world models should not only generate consistent observations, but also maintain world states that persist across agents and evolve across views. Existing autoregressive video diffusion pipelines carry forward observation history as conditioning context, which makes shared state difficult to maintain in multi-agent and multi-view settings. We present WorldWeaver (W^2), a streaming multi-agent video diffusion model that augments rollout with cross-agent world state registers: learnable tokens that store shared world information, track individual agent status, and are dynamically updated after each generated chunk. We ground these registers with supervision signals spanning individual agent status, global state views including bird's-eye views, and scene text. We further improve the architecture with a Mixture-of-Transformers design that uses separate weights for world state modeling and visual frame modeling. Extensive experiments in two-agent Minecraft video generation show that explicit world-state modeling improves logical consistency and generation quality.

</details>


### [217] [3D-Aware VLMs with Implicit and Explicit Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21595)
*Wenhao Li,Xueying Jiang,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Ran Xu,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM-IE3D是一个统一框架，通过从RGB视频中学习隐式和显式3D几何表征（IGT和EGT），并利用3D感知适配器与2D视觉线索融合，在不依赖额外3D输入的情况下，显著增强视觉语言模型在多种3D任务中的细粒度空间理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D视觉输入的视觉语言模型在处理需要细粒度空间理解和推理的3D任务时表现不佳，缺乏有效的3D空间感知能力。

Method: 提出VLM-IE3D框架，包含：(1) 隐式几何标记(IGT)从视频中捕获高层几何先验；(2) 显式几何标记(EGT)从重建的3D属性中编码详细几何结构；(3) 3D感知适配器将两种几何表征与2D视觉线索有效融合，仅需RGB输入即可注入强3D归纳偏置。

Result: 在3D视频检测、3D视觉定位、3D密集字幕生成和空间推理等多种3D任务中，VLM-IE3D一致性地取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: 仅依赖RGB视频输入，通过隐式和显式几何表征的结合，能够有效提升VLMs的3D空间感知能力，为无需额外3D传感器的3D视觉语言理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, most existing vision-language models (VLMs) built from 2D visual inputs often struggle when handling various 3D tasks that require fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning. To bridge this gap, we present VLM-IE3D, a unified framework that enhances the 3D spatial awareness of VLMs by equipping them with both implicit and explicit 3D geometries learned from RGB videos. Our VLM-IE3D introduces Implicit Geometry Tokens (IGTs) that capture high-level geometric priors from input videos, as well as complementary Explicit Geometry Tokens (EGTs) that encode detailed geometric structures from reconstructed 3D attributes. On top of that, VLM-IE3D comes with a 3D-aware adapter that effectively fuses the two types of geometric representations with 2D visual cues. This RGB-only design injects strong 3D inductive biases for fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning without requiring any additional 3D inputs. Extensive experiments show that VLM-IE3D achieves superior performance consistently across various 3D tasks including 3D video detection, 3D visual grounding, 3D dense captioning, and spatial reasoning. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Vegetebird/VLM-IE3D.

</details>


### [218] [Deblurring in the Wild: A Real-World Image Deblurring Dataset from Smartphone High-Speed Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19445)
*Syed Mumtahin Mahmud,Mahdi Mohd Hossain Noki,Prothito Shovon Majumder,Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Sudipto Das Sukanto,Afia Lubaina,Md. Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了目前最大的真实世界图像去模糊数据集，利用智能手机慢动作视频（240帧/秒）模拟长时间曝光模糊，包含42,000+高分辨率模糊-清晰图像对，规模约为现有数据集的10倍，场景多样性提升8倍。实验表明现有SOTA模型在该数据集上性能显著下降，凸显了其作为更具挑战性基准的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊数据集规模有限、场景多样性不足，且多为合成数据，无法充分反映真实世界模糊的复杂性，亟需大规模、高多样性的真实基准数据集以推动模型泛化性和鲁棒性研究。

Method: 利用智能手机慢动作视频（240 fps），在一秒内采集240帧，通过对帧平均模拟长时间曝光生成模糊图像，同时以时间中心帧作为清晰参考，构建了42,000+对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像对，涵盖室内外场景及多种物体与相机运动。

Result: 数据集规模是主流数据集的约10倍（42,000+对），场景多样性提升8倍；多个SOTA去模糊模型在该数据集上性能显著下降，验证了其作为更复杂、更具挑战性基准的有效性。

Conclusion: 该大规模真实世界去模糊数据集为去模糊研究提供了更具挑战性和多样性的新基准，有助于推动鲁棒且泛化能力更强的去模糊模型的发展。

Abstract: We introduce the largest real-world image deblurring dataset constructed from smartphone slow-motion videos. Using 240 frames captured over one second, we simulate realistic long-exposure blur by averaging frames to produce blurry images, while using the temporally centered frame as the sharp reference. Our dataset contains over 42,000 high-resolution blur-sharp image pairs, making it approximately 10 times larger than widely used datasets, with 8 times the amount of different scenes, including indoor and outdoor environments, with varying object and camera motions. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art (SOTA) deblurring models on our dataset and observe significant performance degradation, highlighting the complexity and diversity of our benchmark. Our dataset serves as a challenging new benchmark to facilitate robust and generalizable deblurring models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [AuthProbe: Specification-Driven, Multi-Identity Detection of Broken Object-Level Authorization in Recruitment API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20574)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AuthProbe是一个开源的黑盒扫描器，通过OpenAPI规范和多个身份驱动测试，能够检测HTTP API中的BOLA/IDOR漏洞，在合成测试中实现了零误报，且运行时间与测试对象数量呈线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BOLA/IDOR是OWASP API安全排名首位的漏洞类型，其恶意请求与合法请求在字节级别上不可区分，导致传统WAF和单身份扫描器无法检测。需要一种能够区分合法与恶意请求的检测方法。

Method: 基于OpenAPI规范，使用两个或多个身份同时进行测试：先发现每个身份合法拥有的对象，然后尝试以一个身份读取另一个身份的对象，通过与真实所有者获取的ground-truth响应对比确认泄露；同时遍历可预测标识符检测枚举漏洞，报告缺失的身份验证和存在性预言。

Result: 在包含McHire故障类的合成招聘API上，AuthProbe检测到所有植入的跨身份读取漏洞，对加固版本零误报，运行时间与测试对象数量呈线性关系。

Conclusion: AuthProbe能够有效检测BOLA/IDOR漏洞，可集成到CI/CD流水线中，作为开源工具（Apache 2.0许可）发布，并设有授权使用护栏。

Abstract: Broken Object-Level Authorization (BOLA), also known as Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR), has topped the OWASP API Security ranking since 2019 and is the root cause of some of the largest exposures of applicant data in recruitment technology. The defining feature of this flaw class is that a malicious request is byte-for-byte indistinguishable from a legitimate one, which is precisely why web application firewalls and single identity scanners fail to catch it. We present AuthProbe, an open-source, black-box scanner that detects BOLA and IDOR in HTTP APIs by driving its tests from an OpenAPI specification and by acting under two or more identities that the operator controls. AuthProbe discovers, for each identity, the objects that identity legitimately owns, then attempts to read one identity's objects while authenticated as another and confirms a leak by comparing the response against a ground-truth fetch by the true owner. It also walks predictable identifiers to expose enumeration and reports missing authentication and existence oracles. The tool returns a severity-thresholded exit code and machine-readable reports so that it can gate a continuous integration build. On a synthetic recruitment API in which the McHire failure class is reproduced, AuthProbe detects every planted cross-identity read with no false positives on a hardened counterpart, and its running time grows linearly with the number of objects under test. AuthProbe is released under the Apache 2.0 license with an authorized-use guardrail.

</details>


### [220] [Deepfake News Detection: A Multimodal Framework Integrating LipNet, DeepSpeech and ResNET for Enhanced Audio-Visual Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20579)
*Ameena Khan,Muhammad Ahsan Aziz,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Naeem Akhter,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态框架，通过联合利用唇部运动、音频内容和视频帧特征来检测深度伪造新闻视频，在FakeAVCeleb数据集上达到94%的准确率，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得高保真伪造视频和克隆语音易于获取，严重威胁数字新闻媒体的真实性，因此需要有效的多模态深度伪造检测方法。

Method: 提出多模态框架，使用LipNet和DeepSpeech2编码唇部运动和语音内容，利用BlazeFace提取面部特征并用ResNet18表示，将特征向量拼接为整体视频表示，采用RF、MLP和LSTM等集成分类器进行检测。

Result: 在FakeAVCeleb数据集上，使用增强音频特征达到94%的准确率，超越了最先进的多模态集成基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在深度伪造新闻检测中展现出鲁棒性和实际应用潜力，验证了多模态融合策略的有效性。

Abstract: Deepfake news refers to AI-generated (or AI ma-nipulated) multimedia content intentionally generated to deceive audiences by manipulating the facial expressions, or speech while maintaining the realistic appearance. The rapid progress of generative AI has made the synthesis of highly realistic fake videos and cloned voices widely accessible, posing a serious threat to the authenticity of digital news media. This paper presents a multi-modal framework that discerns the authenticity of video content by jointly exploiting audio and visual cues, thereby addressing the challenge of detecting the deepfake videos. We proposed a framework that involves features extraction from lip movements, audio content and video frames. Lip movements and speech content are encoded using the LipNet and DeepSpeech2 models, while facial features are extracted by leveraging the use of BlazeFace and represented with ResNet18. The extracted feature vectors are concatenated into a holistic video representation and classified with an ensemble of machine learning and deep learning models, including Random Forest (RF), Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Exten-sive experiments performed on the FakeAVCeleb dataset shows that the proposed approach attains an accuracy of 94% using augmented audio features, outperforming a state-of-the-art multi-modal ensemble baseline. The results confirm the robustness and practical potential of the proposed framework for deepfake news detection.

</details>


### [221] [Geometric Configurations of Perturbed Jailbreak Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20581)
*Lynn Delcon,Andres Algaba,Vincent Ginis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对LLM的字符串级扰动越狱攻击的内部表示，发现在Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.2系列小型模型中，最后层最后token嵌入空间和top-50下一个token概率空间中均不存在行为超平面，仅少数特定token与顺从回答显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续演变的扰动技术能够将失败的越狱提示转化为成功的越狱提示，对LLM安全构成严重威胁，因此需要深入理解这类攻击的内部工作机制。

Method: 选取两个表示空间：最后层最后token嵌入空间和top-50下一个token概率空间，在Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.2系列多个尺寸的小型模型上分析字符串级扰动越狱输入的内部表示。

Result: 最后层最后token嵌入空间根据拼写和格式分离提示；top-50概率空间有效但为一维且聚类更复杂。在以拒绝为主的回答集中，两个空间均未发现行为超平面。仅Qwen-1.5B模型的"Sure" token以及Llama-1B模型的","和"ĊĊ" token与顺从回答显著关联。

Conclusion: 在小型模型中，字符串级扰动越狱输入的内部表示并未表现出明显的可分离性，仅在个别token层面存在与顺从回答的关联。

Abstract: Perturbation techniques that turn unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones are continuously evolving, constituting a major security threat to LLM safety. In this paper, we investigate the internal representations of such string-level perturbed jailbreak inputs in the small weight models of the Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct families. We select two representation spaces: the last-layer-last-token embedding space and the top-50 next-token probability space. The former space separates prompts based on their spelling and format, while the latter space is effectively one-dimensional but appears more complex to cluster. Within our refusal-dominated answer set we find no behavioral hyperplane in either space. Only the next token "Sure" in the 1.5B Qwen model, and both tokens "," and "ĊĊ" in the 1$ Llama model, display a significant association with a compliant-labeled answer.

</details>


### [222] [Enhancing Attack Detection Capabilities in BACnet/IP Networks Using Machine-Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20686)
*Derek Manzella,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对BAS中BACnet/IP协议的安全检测问题，提出了三项贡献：改进Zeek解析器生成统一日志、构建基于bacpypes3的仿真测试平台（含物理设备行为、调度逻辑和攻击标签），以及评估五种无监督异常检测模型。实验表明One-Class SVM表现最佳，平均F1为0.864，高流量攻击（DoS、侦察）F1>0.99，但隐蔽攻击（篡改、虚假数据注入）仅约0.77。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前建筑自动化系统（BAS）使用BACnet/IP协议管理关键功能，但防御者缺乏针对BACnet特定攻击的检测工具和标注数据集，现有工具和数据集不足以支持机器学习检测方法的开发与评估。

Method: 1) 修改CISA的Zeek BACnet解析器，生成统一的逐包日志，简化特征工程；2) 使用bacpypes3构建仿真BACnet/IP测试平台，模拟小型商业HVAC系统，包含基于物理的设备行为、调度感知控制器逻辑和逐包攻击标注；3) 基于基线流量和六种BACnet攻击类型（包括DoS、侦察、属性篡改、虚假数据注入），评估五种无监督异常检测模型。

Result: One-Class SVM总体表现最佳，平均F1为0.864。对于高流量DoS和侦察攻击，F1超过0.99；而对于隐蔽性更强的篡改和虚假数据注入攻击，F1约为0.77。高流量攻击的检测效果显著优于隐蔽攻击。

Conclusion: 本文通过改进解析器、构建仿真平台和评估检测模型，填补了BAS中BACnet/IP攻击检测工具和标注数据的空白。One-Class SVM在总体性能上最优，但检测能力在不同攻击类型间存在显著差距，高流量攻击易检测，隐蔽攻击仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Building Automation Systems (BAS) manage critical building functions using protocols such as BACnet/IP, yet defenders have limited tooling and few labeled datasets for detecting BACnet-specific attacks. This work addresses these gaps through three contributions. First, CISA's Zeek BACnet parser is modified to produce a unified per-packet log, simplifying feature engineering for machine-learning (ML) pipelines. Second, a simulated BACnet/IP testbed is developed using bacpypes3 to model a small commercial HVAC system with physics-based device behavior, schedule-aware controller logic, and per-packet attack labeling. Third, five unsupervised anomaly detection models are evaluated using baseline traffic and six BACnet attack types, including denial of service, reconnaissance, property tampering, and false data injection. Results show that One-Class SVM achieved the strongest overall performance, with an average F1 score of 0.864 across all attacks and F1 scores above 0.99 for high-volume denial-of-service and reconnaissance attacks. Detection is much stronger for high-volume attacks, such as DoS attacks and reconnaissance, than stealthier techniques such as tampering and false data injection, which scored around 77%.

</details>


### [223] [Buzz to Boom: Detecting Message Progression Vulnerabilities in Electron Applications via Segmented Directed Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20698)
*Jianjia Yu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yu Sun,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Proton，一种分段定向模糊测试框架，用于检测Electron应用中的消息推进漏洞（MPVs）。该方法将端到端模糊测试沿进程间消息边界分解为逐段测试，分别以到达当前进程的敏感目标或传播载荷至下一进程为目标。在589个真实Electron应用中发现了23个零日MPVs，其中22个导致操作系统命令执行，并获得13个确认、11个修复和11个CVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Electron应用中存在跨进程消息传递导致的消息推进漏洞（MPVs），攻击者可通过多步跨进程利用实现权限提升（如命令执行）。现有研究仅关注不安全配置和恶意DOM内容，无法检测或利用这类需要复杂跨进程消息触发漏洞。

Method: 提出Proton分段定向模糊测试框架：将端到端模糊测试沿消息传递边界分解为逐段测试，每段目标为（i）到达当前进程的敏感sink，或（ii）将载荷传播至下一进程以探索另一进程。后者的消息作为下一段的种子语料，最终从各段合成崩溃输入以验证端到端利用。

Result: 在589个真实Electron应用中检测到23个零日MPVs，其中22个可导致OS命令执行（包括GitHub星标超50k的项目），获得13个确认、11个修复、11个CVE及来自Vercel的漏洞赏金。

Conclusion: Proton有效填补了Electron安全中跨进程消息漏洞检测的空白，证明了分段定向模糊测试在发现复杂多步跨进程漏洞方面的有效性。

Abstract: Electron is a popular framework for building cross-platform desktop applications using web technologies. Such applications consist of multiple processes with different privilege levels that communicate via message passing. When inter-process messages carry attacker-controlled inputs, they can propagate across processes and reach privileged APIs, e.g., command execution. Such a message propagation behavior is characterized as Message Progression Vulnerabilities (MPVs). The exploitation of MPVs is challenging because it often requires multiple steps, e.g., first arbitrary code execution in one process via message passing, and then command injection in another process using another message crafted in the first process. To our knowledge, existing works on Electron security only study unsafe configurations and malicious Document Object Model (DOM) content, i.e., they cannot detect or exploit these vulnerabilities that need to be triggered by complex cross-process exploits via message passing. We present Proton, a segmented directed fuzzing framework for detecting MPVs. Our key insight is to decompose end-to-end fuzzing into per-process segments along message-passing boundaries, where the goals of fuzzing each segment are either: (i) reaching a sink in the current process or (ii) propagating the payload to the next process, to enable the exploration of another process. In the second case, the messages seed the corpus of the next segment. Finally, Proton synthesizes crash inputs from each process to validate end-to-end exploits. We evaluate Proton against 589 real-world Electron applications, resulting in 23 zero-day MPVs. Among them, 22 lead to OS command execution, including projects with over 50k GitHub stars. We responsibly disclosed all findings. To date, we have received 13 acknowledgments, 11 fixes, and 11 CVEs, including a bug bounty from Vercel.

</details>


### [224] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Symbolic Security Protocol Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20712)
*Paolo Modesti,Syed Ahmed,Ioannis Sfyrakis,Derek Enodolomwanyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM（GPT和DeepSeek）在安全协议验证任务上的表现，与ProVerif和OFMC等形式化工具进行对比。Chat模式召回率高（69-81%）但精度低（<31%）；推理模式精度提升（GPT 66.5%，DeepSeek 45.4%）但检测能力减半。所有模型在认证目标上表现最差，机密性例外（F1最高95.7%）。结果不稳定，置信度与正确性无关。LLM尚无法匹敌形式化验证，最多可作为预筛选工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（LLM）是否能够替代或辅助形式化工具（如ProVerif、OFMC）进行安全协议验证，评估其在自动化安全分析中的可行性与局限性。

Method: 在130个混淆后的AnB/AnBx协议（涵盖388个安全目标）上，对GPT和DeepSeek的Chat模式与推理模式进行三轮测试，以ProVerif和OFMC的结果为基准，评估召回率、精度、F1等指标。

Result: Chat模型召回率69-81%但精度低于31%；推理模式精度提升（GPT 66.5%，DeepSeek 45.4%）但攻击检测率仅过半。认证目标检测最差（推理模式检测不到半数攻击），机密性表现最好（推理模式F1达95.7%）。结果跨轮次不稳定，模型置信度与正确性无相关性。

Conclusion: LLM当前无法达到形式化验证工具的可靠性，最多可作为预筛选过滤器使用。

Abstract: Security protocol verification relies on formal tools such as ProVerif and OFMC. This study evaluates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform comparable analysis. We test GPT and DeepSeek in chat and reasoning modes over three runs on 130 obfuscated AnB/AnBx protocols covering 388 security goals, scored against ProVerif and OFMC. Chat models reach 69 to 81% recall at precision below 31%. Reasoning models reverse this trade-off, reaching 66.5% precision for GPT and 45.4% for DeepSeek, but detect just over half the attacks. DeepSeek's two modes share one underlying model, so the comparison isolates reasoning itself, which raises precision from 27.2% to 45.4%. The GPT contrast spans a model-version change and is only suggestive. All models perform worst on authentication goals: reasoning models detect well under half of injective and non-injective agreement attacks, whereas chat models over-flag them at low precision. Confidentiality is the exception, with F1 up to 95.7% in reasoning mode. Verdicts are unstable across runs, identical on 89.7% of goals for GPT but 74.0% for DeepSeek. Self-reported confidence is uniformly high yet shows no meaningful correlation with correctness. On this benchmark LLMs do not match formal verification, but may serve, at best, as pre-screening filters.

</details>


### [225] [Security Vulnerability Patterns in AI-Generated Code: A Cross-Model Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20713)
*Shanna M. Kahn,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM生成的自动化脚本普遍存在可利用漏洞，且风险不特定于某个模型，而是与任务类别相关，组织应避免未经审查就部署此类代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专业用户使用基于LLM的编码工具生成日常自动化脚本，这些脚本可能未经安全审查就进入企业工作流，存在严重安全隐患，本研究旨在直接评估这一风险。

Method: 从ChatGPT、Microsoft Copilot和Google Gemini三个平台，使用相同提示在三个自动化领域收集代码；由Claude Code进行标准化漏洞审查；每个漏洞使用CVSS v3.1评分，并映射到OWASP Top 10:2021和MITRE ATT&CK框架。

Result: 每个脚本都存在可利用漏洞；17个漏洞类别中9个出现在所有三个模型的代码中，14个出现在至少两个模型中；跨平台的加权CVSS评分差异小于10%。

Conclusion: 风险不特定于某个特定模型，而是与任务类别相关。组织不应询问该信任哪个工具，而应询问LLM生成的自动化代码是否应该未经审查就部署。

Abstract: LLM-based coding tools enable non-expert users to generate routine automation scripts that may enter enterprise workflows without meaningful security review. This study examines that risk directly. Code was collected from ChatGPT, Microsoft Copilot, and Google Gemini using identical prompts across three automation domains. Claude Code performed a standardized vulnerability review. Each identified vulnerability was scored using CVSS v3.1 and mapped to the OWASP Top 10:2021 and the MITRE ATT&CK frameworks. Every script contained exploitable vulnerabilities. Nine of the 17 identified vulnerability classes appeared in code from all three models, while 14 of the 17 vulnerability classes appeared in at least two models. The weighted CVSS scores across platforms differed by less than 10%. The risk is not tied to any particular model but rather to the task category. Organizations should therefore ask not which tool to trust, but instead whether LLM-generated automation code should be deployed without review.

</details>


### [226] [Leaky Language Models: Stealing Architecture and Inference Optimizations via Per-Token Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20723)
*Sadegh Majidi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Kazem Taram*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeakyLMs提出一系列攻击方法，仅通过令牌生成时序即可从远程API中泄露专有语言模型的架构、部署信息（如推测解码使用情况、草稿模型上下文长度）以及关键架构参数（层数、隐藏维度、注意力头数），在Llama模型上top-10猜测准确率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未充分探索通过远程API的令牌生成时序泄露专有模型信息（架构、部署策略）的可能性，亟需揭示此类侧信道攻击的威胁。

Method: 提出两类核心攻击：1）推理优化与部署策略攻击，通过时序特征检测推测解码等优化技术并推断草稿模型上下文长度；2）架构属性恢复攻击，构建现代NVIDIA GPU上令牌生成延迟的精确时序模型，刻画延迟随模型配置和硬件参数的变化规律，再基于该时序模型在架构空间中进行搜索。

Result: 成功检测到Google Gemini Flash 2.5使用推测解码且草稿模型上下文窗口约128K令牌；Llama模型实验中，近正确架构配置在top-10猜测中占比超90%。

Conclusion: 仅凭令牌生成时序即可有效攻击专有语言模型，泄露其部署策略与核心架构信息，对模型安全性构成严重威胁。

Abstract: This work presents LeakyLMs, a set of attacks that leak proprietary model, architecture, and deployment information from production language models. LeakyLMs is the first to demonstrate that key model and deployment details can be inferred using only token generation timing, even when interacting through remote APIs. LeakyLMs introduces two core attacks. The first attack targets inference optimizations and deployment strategies. For example, our attack detects whether a provider uses speculative decoding, a widely deployed inference-time optimization, and further identifies the context length of the draft model used in the pipeline. Our measurements show that Google Gemini Flash 2.5 uses speculative decoding with a draft context window of approximately 128K tokens. The second attack recovers key architectural properties, including the number of transformer layers, hidden dimension size, and number of attention heads. To achieve this, LeakyLMs builds a detailed and accurate model of token-generation timing on modern NVIDIA GPUs, characterizing how latency scales with model configuration and hardware parameters. The attack then performs a search over the architecture space using this timing model. In experiments with Llama models, the near-correct architectural configuration appears in the top-10 guesses more than 90% of the time.

</details>


### [227] [GPE: Evaluating Robust Evidence Aggregation for Fact Verification under Controllable GEO-Style Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20730)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Xiaomei Yuan,Pengtao Kou,Jiamou Liu,Zhen Li,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在检索证据时可能遭受生成引擎优化（GEO）投毒攻击的问题，本文提出GPE评估框架，包含多领域事实验证基准和可控证据环境，实验表明该方法能暴露模型在对抗性证据下的鲁棒性退化与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地使用搜索工具检索最新信息，但检索到的文档可能被人为操纵（GEO投毒）。现有的事实验证基准和评估框架缺乏可控的证据环境来评估模型在此类攻击下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出GPE框架，包括：(1) 多领域事实验证基准；(2) 用于控制证据来源和投毒比例的评估框架。通过多种验证方法和投毒攻击进行实验。

Result: 实验表明GPE能够暴露模型在对抗性证据环境下的鲁棒性退化和效率权衡，这些现象在纯净评估中无法被观测到。

Conclusion: 证实了在对抗性证据环境下评估事实验证的必要性，GPE为评估模型对GEO投毒的鲁棒性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly use search tools to retrieve up-to-date information, introducing a new attack surface in which retrieved documents can be manipulated. This risk is amplified by the development of generative engine optimization, which can make selected content more likely to be retrieved, cited, and adopted by models. Existing fact-verification benchmarks and evaluation frameworks do not provide the controlled evidence environments needed to assess robustness against GEO poisoning. We therefore propose GPE, which consists of a multi-domain fact-verification benchmark and an evaluation framework for controlling evidence sources and poisoning ratios. Experiments across multiple verification methods and poisoning attacks demonstrate that GPE exposes robustness degradation and efficiency trade-offs that cannot be observed through clean evaluation alone, confirming the need to evaluate fact verification under adversarial evidence environments.

</details>


### [228] [IssueTrojanBench: Benchmarking AI Coding Agents Against Malicious Issue Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20759)
*Ankur Singh,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前最先进的AI编码智能体（Cursor、Claude Code、Codex Desktop）面对恶意Issue请求的安全性。作者构建了IssueTrojanBench基准，包含基于四种新型攻击类别、六种投递向量及扰动增强的恶意Issue。实验发现66.5%的恶意Issue能够穿透所有防护，且拒绝几乎完全来自LLM层面而非智能体框架，GPT模型普遍易受影响，而Sonnet 4.6表现出更有选择性的风险感知阻断。研究强调需要更强有力的智能体和模型级安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体在真实软件开发中日益普及，具备生成、编辑和执行代码的能力，并可自主访问本地文件与工具。这类智能体同时继承了LLM后端的安全风险（如对抗性提示、投毒训练数据、后门触发器）以及智能体架构本身的风险（工具使用自主性可能导致外部API滥用、数据泄露和开发环境持续受损）。目前缺乏对恶意Issue请求的系统性评估，亟需揭示现有防护机制的漏洞。

Method: 作者构建了IssueTrojanBench基准测试集，包含基于四种新型攻击类别（嵌入为Issue中的恶意指令）、六种投递向量（如PDF、Issue评论）并经过扰动增强的恶意Issue。选取当前主流编码智能体（Cursor、Claude Code、Codex Desktop）及其背后的两大模型家族（OpenAI GPT-5.3 Codex/GPT-5.4和Anthropic Sonnet 4.6）进行系统性评估。

Result: 66.5%的恶意Issue能够穿透智能体和LLM层面的所有防护。拒绝阻断几乎完全来自LLM层面而非智能体框架；GPT模型普遍易受攻击，Sonnet 4.6则表现出更有选择性的风险感知阻断能力。当前智能体层面的防御策略提供的额外保护能力有限。

Conclusion: 当前AI编码智能体的防御机制存在严重漏洞，亟需加强智能体和模型层面的安全机制。LLM层面的安全过滤是主要防线，但远不足以应对恶意Issue攻击，需构建更完善的层级防御体系。

Abstract: AI coding agents powered by LLMs are increasingly integrated into real-world software development, where they generate, edit, and execute code with autonomous access to local files and tools. Coding agents inherit security risks from both the LLM backbone, where adversarial prompts, poisoned training data, and backdoor triggers can cause models to emit insecure or attacker-chosen code, and their agentic architecture, where tool-using autonomy enables induced misuse of external APIs, data exfiltration, and persistent compromise of development environments. This paper presents a systematic evaluation of malicious issue requests against state-of-the-art coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex Desktop), powered by two major model families (OpenAI GPT-5.3 Codex/GPT-5.4 and Anthropic Sonnet 4.6). Our novel benchmark IssueTrojanBench contains malicious issues that are constructed based on four novel attack categories (i.e., embedded as malicious instructions in issues), six delivery vectors (e.g., PDF, or issue comment), and further augmented by perturbations. Our results reveal critical vulnerabilities in the as-deployed modern coding agents, i.e., 66.5% of the malicious issues from IssueTrojanBench penetrate all the guardrails (agent- and LLM-level) of coding agents. Our further analysis shows that rejection is almost entirely from LLMs rather than the agent frameworks, with GPT models broadly vulnerable and Sonnet 4.6 exhibiting more selective, risk-aware blocking of high-impact actions. Our evaluation also highlights that the current agent-level defense strategy offers limited additional protection for coding agents. Our findings highlight the urgent need for stronger agent- and model-level safety mechanisms to protect AI coding agents.

</details>


### [229] [Classical Acceptance Is Not Hybrid Authentication: Measuring X.509 Verifier Semantics in Post-Quantum Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20800)
*Taesung Kim,Boheung Chung,Keonwoo Kim,Yousung Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 混合X.509证书验证存在结构性缺陷：可分离设计允许仅基于经典路径接受证书，而忽略后量子证据。在8个验证栈、9种模式、6种证书方案中，几乎所有栈在混合必需策略下默认仅验证经典路径；后量子凭证撤销时验证仍通过，因为绑定凭证不在决策范围内。绑定成功不等于认证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合X.509证书同时携带经典和后量子凭证，但可分离设计使验证者可能仅基于经典路径接受证书，后量子证据从未影响决策，导致经典结果被错误提升为混合结论。本文旨在测量这一结构性缺陷并诊断其根源。

Method: 对8个路径验证栈（7个独立代码库）、9种验证模式、6种证书方案进行实证测量。提出规范导出的验证者模型和可执行、策略参数化的参考合约。

Result: 混合必需策略下，几乎所有栈解析可分离混合证书时仅基于经典路径接受；强化模式因签名输入编码尚未互操作而破坏互操作性；验证后量子签名的栈默认不强制绑定；后量子凭证撤销时默认路径仍接受。

Conclusion: 混合X.509证书验证存在结构性降级风险：验证者必须识别、验证、使后量子证据成为结果导向的、并在报告混合验证前进行检查。当前标准未要求这些，需要修正。

Abstract: A relying party validating a hybrid X.509 certificate --- carrying both a classical and a post-quantum credential --- must distinguish whether its accepting judgment rests on the post-quantum evidence or only on the classical path. To preserve compatibility, the separable designs place that evidence where classical path validation may ignore it. A verifier can then validate the classical path and accept while the post-quantum evidence never bears on the decision --- a valid classical result silently promoted to a hybrid conclusion it did not establish. We measure this across eight path-validation stacks (seven independent codebases), in nine validation modes, over six certificate schemes. Under a hybrid-required policy, nearly every stack parsing a separable hybrid certificate accepts on the classical path without making the post-quantum evidence outcome-bearing; one enforcing mode instead fractures interoperability over a signature-input encoding not yet interoperably profiled; and stacks that verify post-quantum signatures still do not enforce the binding by default: the gap is structural, not explained by missing primitive capability alone. Under lifecycle desynchronization the downgrade is realized: when a bound post-quantum credential is revoked while the classical certificate stays valid, the default path still accepts, because the bound credential lies outside the decision's scope. Binding success is not authentication success. We contribute a specification-derived verifier model and an executable, policy-parametric reference contract --- what a verifier must recognize, verify, make outcome-bearing, and check before reporting a validation as hybrid --- with a diagnosis of why standards do not require it.

</details>


### [230] [Which Model Is Actually Serving You? IRIS: Budgeted Black-Box Auditing of Model Substitution and Routing Dilution in LLM Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20860)
*Yuewei Zhang,Zhi-Hai Zhang,Hanzhang Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IRIS是一种仅依赖返回文本的审计方法，通过让端点生成随机数或字符串来识别LLM网关的后端模型，能够同时检测完整流替代和分数稀释、归因后端、估计路由分数ε，并自适应确定查询预算，在多种场景下达到高检测率和精确的ε估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM网关可能暗中替换后端模型（如使用更便宜的模型替代广告中的模型，或仅将部分请求路由至广告模型），现有的黑盒审计方法通常需要特权信号（如对数概率、token排名或参考样本）、预先固定查询预算，且仅能给出是/否判定，缺乏一种仅依赖文本输出的综合性审计方案。

Method: 提出IRIS审计方法：1) 让端点生成随机数或字符串作为指纹；2) 结合检测完整流替代和分数稀释；3) 归因后端模型；4) 估计路由分数ε；5) 通过廉价预实验拟合指数级查询-误差衰减曲线，在发出疑似查询前自动冻结预算大小。

Result: 在Qwen3家族模型上AUROC达0.99；在OpenRouter库上对ε=0.3的稀释检测平均统计功效0.85（假阳性率0.017），ε估计误差在0.04以内；跨提供商审计中14/15个同模型提供商对被检测出量化及核差异；与现有黑盒审计相比匹配或超越检测性能，自适应分配将匹配预算的目标命中率从73%提升至87%。

Conclusion: IRIS是首个仅靠文本输出就能同时完成完整流替代检测、分数稀释检测、后端归因、路由分数估计以及自适应预算确定的综合审计方法，在多种实验条件下表现优异，并进一步验证了对对抗性网关、旋钮可识别性、未见稀释剂及假阳性控制的有效性。

Abstract: Commercial LLM gateways mediate access to hosted models, but the served backend may not match the advertised one: it may substitute a cheaper model on every request or route only a fraction $ε$ of requests to it. Prior black-box auditors often need a privileged signal (log-probabilities, token ranks, or reference samples) or a target-specific probe, fix the query budget in advance, and return a yes/no verdict. We present $\mathrm{IRIS}$, an audit that needs only the returned text: it asks endpoints to generate random numbers or strings, fingerprints the backend, and is the first to combine, in one text-only audit, detection of whole-stream substitution and fractional dilution, attribution of the served backend, routing-fraction ($ε$) estimation, and a query budget it sizes itself. A cheap pilot fits the exponential query-error decay and freezes that budget before any suspect query is issued. On an intra-family Qwen3 ladder $\mathrm{IRIS}$ verifies the backend at $0.99$ AUROC and sharpens attribution as queries accumulate; across a commercial OpenRouter library it catches $ε{=}0.3$ dilution on margin-qualified pairs at $0.85$ mean power ($0.017$ false-positive rate) and recovers $ε$ to within $0.04$ for enrolled diluents; and a live cross-provider audit flags $14$ of $15$ same-model provider pairs by genuine quantization and kernel deviations, corroborated on third-party MET traces. Against comparable black-box auditors, $\mathrm{IRIS}$ matches or beats detection on shared tasks, and adaptive allocation lifts the matched-budget target-hit rate from $73$% to $87$%. Further experiments cover adversarial gateways, knob identifiability, unseen diluents, and false-positive control.

</details>


### [231] [Toward cryptographically verifiable authorization for autonomous AI agents: A security hypothesis, preliminary formal model, and proof-of-concept implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21325)
*M. Llambí-Morillas,D. Fernández-Fernández*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CVA（密码学可验证的智能体授权）的正式抽象框架，将智能体授权形式化为一个密码学可验证的关系，该关系同时绑定智能体主体、具体授权请求、执行上下文及策略满足性，同时选择性保护私有授权属性的机密性。论文定义了核心安全属性（授权可靠性、主体绑定、请求绑定、策略绑定、重放抵抗），并基于Groth16 zk-SNARK构建了可执行零知识证明概念验证。论文进一步识别并形式化了身份绑定、授权请求绑定与运行时执行绑定之间的结构性分离，将其定位为智能体系统安全设计中的核心开放问题，并提出了可证伪的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证和授权机制虽能建立身份和委派权限，但无法提供密码学证据来证明特定智能体发出的具体请求在特定执行上下文中满足适用的策略。这一缺失使得在有限人类监督下运行的自主AI代理缺乏可验证的安全保障。

Method: 提出CVA形式化抽象，定义安全属性集，利用Groth16 zk-SNARK构建零知识证明概念验证系统，并形式化分析身份绑定、授权请求绑定与运行时执行绑定之间的结构性分离问题。

Result: 构建了CVA的初步形式化抽象和可执行零知识证明概念验证系统，识别并形式化了身份绑定、授权请求绑定与运行时执行绑定的结构性分离这一核心开放问题。

Conclusion: 当前智能体安全框架未明确处理身份、授权请求与执行上下文之间的结构性分离问题，需要进一步研究解决这一核心开放问题，论文提出了可证伪的研究议程作为未来方向。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly execute actions, invoke tools, and operate on protected resources with limited human oversight. Existing authentication and authorization mechanisms establish identity and delegate authority, but do not inherently provide cryptographic evidence that a concrete request issued by a specific agent satisfies the applicable policy in a specific execution context. This paper hypothesizes that agent authorization can be formalized as a cryptographically verifiable relation, denoted $R_{CVA}$, that jointly binds an agent principal, a concrete authorization request, an execution context, and the satisfaction of an applicable policy, while selectively preserving the confidentiality of private authorization attributes. We introduce a preliminary formal abstraction for Cryptographically Verifiable Agent Authorization (CVA), define a compact set of candidate security properties including authorization soundness, principal binding, request binding, policy binding, and replay resistance, and provide an executable zero-knowledge proof of concept that instantiates selected elements of the model over a Groth16 zk-SNARK construction. We further identify and formalize the structural separation among identity binding, authorization-request binding, and runtime execution binding as a central open problem in the design of secure agentic systems (a distinction {not explicitly addressed by} current agentic security frameworks) and present a falsifiable research agenda for its resolution.

</details>


### [232] [Themis Consensus Extension v1: MEV Mitigation by Randomized Delayed Execution and Intent-Hiding Transactions in Application-Specific Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21406)
*Shoeb Siddiqui,Mateusz Nowakowski,Stanislav Vozarik,Gleb Urvanov,Peter Kris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了Mangata于2021年首次发布的Themis Consensus Extension v1协议，该协议将MEV中的重排序提取(VER)与拒绝提取(VED)分离。VER通过连续区块生产者协作实现可验证的确定性随机排列；VED通过加密交易隐藏意图。在自利但非共谋验证者、安全密码学等假设下，该设计限制了单方面排序控制并隐藏交易意图，但不提供完全的抗审查或公平性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）源于特权参与者对待定交易的选择、排除、插入或重排序以谋取私利。现有共识机制无法有效约束这种价值提取行为，需要设计专门的共识扩展来分离和限制不同类型的MEV。

Method: 将MEV分离为VER（重排序提取）和VED（拒绝提取）。VER采用连续区块生产者机制：一个生产者提交交易集，下一个生产者生成可验证的确定性随机种子，执行种子依赖且保持依赖关系的排列。VED采用加密交易机制：用户为指定构建者和执行者加密交易，构建者移除外层并提交不透明密文，执行者仅在承诺后揭示并执行明文。

Result: 在自利但非共谋验证者、低于底层共识故障阈值、安全密码学和可问责角色执行条件下，该设计限制了单方面提交后排序控制权，并向中继和构建者隐藏交易意图。不提供发送顺序或接收顺序公平性、完全抗审查、抗构建者-执行者共谋、或每笔交易的价格保证。

Conclusion: 分析了概率提取、垃圾交易、依赖交易、解密活跃性、会话边界、完全拒绝和阈值共谋等问题。记录了基于Aura的Substrate初始实现及其向BABE-based sr25519/VRF种子路径的过渡，包括延迟执行、Fisher-Yates洗牌和Xoshiro256++。该成果在保留原始提议的同时，将其主张限定在明确假设范围内。

Abstract: Maximal extractable value (MEV) arises when privileged participants select, exclude, insert, or reorder pending transactions for private gain. We specify and analyze the Themis Consensus Extension v1, first published by Mangata in 2021. The design separates value extraction by reordering (VER) from value extraction by denial (VED). For VER, block construction and execution occur across consecutive producers: one producer commits a transaction set, and the next derives a publicly verifiable, deterministic, previously un- predictable seed and executes a seed-determined, dependency-preserving permutation. For selective VED, a user may encrypt a transaction for a designated builder and executor. The builder removes an outer layer and commits the opaque inner ciphertext; the executor reveals and executes the plaintext only after commitment. Under selfish but non-colluding validators, an adversary below the underlying consensus fault threshold, secure cryptography, and accountable role performance, the construction limits unilateral post-commit ordering control and hides transaction intent from relays and the builder. It does not provide send-order or receive-order fairness, complete censorship resistance, resistance to builder-executor collusion, or per-transaction price guarantees. We analyze probabilistic extraction, spam, dependent transactions, decryption liveness, session boundaries, total denial, and threshold coalitions. We also document the initial Aura-based Substrate implementation and its subsequent transition to a BABE-based sr25519/VRF seed path, together with delayed execution, Fisher-Yates shuffling, and Xoshiro256++. The result preserves the original proposal while narrowing its claims to explicit assumptions.

</details>


### [233] [Where You Tap Matters: A Probe-and-Model Benchmark for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21564)
*Gabriele Oligeri,Savio Sciancalepore,Ingrid Huso,Fatima Al-Mousawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了BPSK接收链中五个探针点对基于重构误差的开集RFFI性能的影响，发现探针选择（尤其是定时恢复和载波恢复）主导性能，远超过自动编码器复杂度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFFI文献对接收链中采样点的选择缺乏一致性，不同接收操作（载波恢复、增益归一化、脉冲成形、定时恢复）对信号施加不同变换，可能收紧发射机内变异性或抑制RFFI所需的特征，因此需要系统评估探针点选择对RFFI性能的影响。

Method: 在标准BPSK接收链的五个探针点收集数据，进行基于重构误差的开集RFFI系统评估；通过控制预处理和MSE评分的流水线，基准测试多个LLM设计的自动编码器架构，验证发现的有效性。

Result: RFFI强烈依赖于探针点选择：定时恢复和载波恢复可实现低误接受率（FAR）操作且类内-类外重叠有限；其他阶段通常需要FAR>0.1才能达到0.9的真接受率（TAR）；LLM设计的自动编码器未能在选定操作点超越基线，且通常增加训练时间。

Conclusion: 探针选择主导基于重构的开集RFFI性能，其影响远大于自动编码器复杂度的作用。

Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprint Identification (RFFI) enables transmitter identification at the physical layer by learning device-specific impairments from received signals, yet the literature is inconsistent about where in the receiver chain those samples should be collected. Since distinct transformations are applied to the signal by the different receiver operations, i.e., carrier recovery, gain normalization, pulse shaping, and timing recovery, they can either tighten within-transmitter variability or suppress the features RFFI requires for classification. We present a systematic real-world evaluation of open-set, reconstruction-error RFFI using data collected at five probe points along a standard BPSK receiver chain. Our results show that RFFI is strongly probe-dependent: timing recovery and, to a lesser extent, carrier recovery enable low false-acceptance operation with limited in-distribution-out-of-distribution overlap, whereas other stages often require a false-acceptance ratio above 0.1 to achieve a true-acceptance ratio of 0.9. To test the validity of our findings across model selection, we benchmark several LLM-designed autoencoders using a controlled pipeline that holds preprocessing and MSE scoring fixed. These architectures confirm that RFFI is probe-dependent. Moreover, they do not outperform the baseline at the chosen operating point and typically increase training time. Overall, probe selection dominates reconstruction-based open-set RFFI performance, more than the autoencoder complexity.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [SPECTRA: State-Space Exogenous Context and Temporal-Frequency Resolution Architecture for Probabilistic Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20587)
*Hang Ye,Xinyan Jiang,Yuedong Shi,Yangxin Zhu,Jianming Wei,Tian Zheng,Xiaoying Zheng,Yongxin Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构，通过自适应分离确定性趋势与高频残差流，结合多分辨率谱域-时域建模与有序分位数估计，在负荷、电价、光伏和风电四种概率预测任务中，18个设置下14个取得最优连续排序概率评分（CRPS），平均降低5.74%，上尾分位数风险降低7.27%，验证了确定性-随机性分离作为通用概率能源预测设计原则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统面临可再生能源间歇性、柔性负荷、市场波动和天气相关发电等多重交互不确定性，亟需概率预测。但现有方法通常将多尺度分解、外生变量对齐和概率输出作为独立步骤处理，未能揭示可预测结构与不确定性波动如何共同塑造预测分布。

Method: 提出一种状态空间外生上下文与时频分辨率架构：1）自适应分离确定性流（趋势-周期分量）和残差流（高频波动）；2）将外生上下文与两种流对齐；3）通过多分辨率谱域-时域状态空间建模优化确定性主干；4）从互补表示中估计有序分位数边界。

Result: 在负荷、电价、光伏和风电四种预测任务的18个设置中，14个取得最佳CRPS，平均CRPS降低5.74%，上尾分位数风险降低7.27%。

Conclusion: 确定性-随机性分离可作为通用概率能源预测的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Modern power systems increasingly require probabilistic forecasts amid interacting uncertainties from renewable intermittency, flexible demand, market volatility, and weather-dependent generation. However, existing methods often treat multi-scale decomposition, exogenous-variable alignment, and probabilistic output as separate steps, obscuring how predictable structures and uncertainty-bearing fluctuations jointly shape the forecast distribution. This paper proposes a state-space exogenous-context and temporal-frequency resolution architecture for general probabilistic energy forecasting. Its central premise is that trend-periodic components primarily determine the baseline trajectory, whereas high-frequency residuals and external perturbations govern the spread and asymmetry of forecast uncertainty. Accordingly, the architecture adaptively separates deterministic and residual streams, aligns exogenous context with both, refines the deterministic backbone through multi-resolution spectral-temporal state-space modeling, and estimates ordered quantile boundaries from their complementary representations. Experiments on load, price, solar, and wind forecasting achieve the best continuous ranked probability score in 14 of 18 settings, reducing average CRPS by 5.74\% and upper-tail quantile risk by 7.27\% over the strongest baselines. These results support deterministic-stochastic separation as an effective design principle for general probabilistic energy forecasting.

</details>


### [235] [Automatic knot selection in smooth additive models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21083)
*Nicolás Carrizosa,Vanesa Guerrero,María Durbán*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应样条（A-splines）扩展的显式结选择技术，结合定制的Fellner-Schall调参方案，用于广义可加模型（GAMs）。相比P-splines等传统方法，该方法在保持相当性能的同时，能显著减少基函数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B样条回归中结（knots）的数量和位置选择对模型性能至关重要，但现有方法存在不足：传统结选择算法常因计算或建模限制被忽视，而正则化方法（如P-splines）虽已标准化但仍有改进空间。本文旨在开发一种兼顾性能与模型简洁性的显式结选择方法。

Method: 基于自适应样条（A-splines）结选择方法的扩展，结合定制的Fellner-Schall方案进行参数调优，应用于广义可加模型（GAMs）框架。

Result: 在多种合成和真实数据集上的评估表明，该方法与P-splines及现有最优结选择技术性能相当，但模型所用的基函数数量显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的显式结选择技术在保持预测性能的同时，能构建更简洁的模型（基函数更少），在某些场景下相比正则化方法具有优势。

Abstract: B-spline regression constitutes a widely used framework for nonparametric modeling. The performance of this methodology depends on specifying the number and placement of changepoints, known as knots, prior to the estimation process. Such knot sequence determines the dimension of the B-spline basis used to represent the regression function and the number of coefficients to be estimated. Therefore, the knots' choice affects the model's flexibility, influencing its smoothness and goodness-of-fit. Traditionally, this problem has been addressed either by explicitly selecting knots, via knot-selection algorithms, or by regularization methods, such as P-splines, which automatically tune the regressor's smoothness. The latter have become the standard in generalized additive models (GAMs). In contrast, knot-selection techniques, frequently neglected because of computational or modeling limitations, provide certain advantages which can be valuable in some contexts. In this work, we introduce a novel explicit knot-selection technique for GAMs based on an extension of the adaptive splines (A-splines) knot selection methodology, combined with a customized Fellner-Schall scheme for tuning the associated parameters. Our approach is evaluated on various synthetic and real datasets and compared with P-splines and state-of-the-art knot-selection techniques. The results indicate comparable performance, while producing models built on a substantially smaller number of basis elements.

</details>


### [236] [Transformer-based Diffusion models for Hydrological Time Series Probabilistic Imputation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21200)
*Ferdinand Bhavsar,Lionel Benoit,Maxime Savatier,Edith Gabriel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的扩散模型，用于有限观测下水文气象时间序列的建模、插补和预测。在法国东北部六个站点15年以上的水文水质数据上验证，该方法优于传统基线模型，能有效捕捉复杂时序特征，在缺失数据场景下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文气象时间序列观测数据有限且高度可变，传统统计方法难以准确刻画其动态特征，亟需更强大的建模方法来提升对水文系统的监测与洪水/干旱风险评估能力。

Method: 采用基于Transformer架构的扩散模型，对多站点水文时间序列进行联合建模。模型在六个相邻源头集水区的水量和水质数据上进行了校准与验证（数据经过15年以上质量控制和传感器漂移校正），并与多种常用基线方法进行对比。

Result: 定量评估表明，Transformer扩散模型在时间序列插补（缺失数据填充）和预测（未来水文条件预测）两个任务上均表现有效，能够高效采样真实的时间序列分布，在可变缺失数据设置下优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: Transformer扩散模型能够有效捕捉和模拟水文数据中的复杂模式，为有限观测下的水文时间序列建模提供了有前景的新方向，尤其在缺失数据场景下展现出鲁棒性和生成能力。

Abstract: The modeling of hydrometeorological time series with limited observations is a key challenge in the monitoring of hydro-systems and water resources, as well as for flood or drought risk assessment. Due to the high variability of the underlying processes and the sparsity of available measurements, traditional statistical approaches often struggle to accurately represent their dynamics. In this context, recent advances in deep learning offer a promising direction for improving the representation and generation of complex temporal processes sampled at several observation sites. This study investigates the application of transformer-based diffusion models to the simulation and reconstruction of hydrological time series. The proposed framework is applied to the joint modeling of water quantity and quality at six sites spread across three adjacent headwater catchments located in North-East France on a limestone plateau covered by forests and field crops. The model is calibrated and validated using available observational data, which has been quality controlled and corrected for sensor drift and malfunction through collaborative efforts by LNE metrology expertise and Andra monthly quality control over more than 15 years. Its performance is compared with several established baseline approaches commonly used for time series modeling. Quantitative evaluation metrics are employed to assess the ability of the proposed method to reproduce key temporal characteristics of the observed signals in two settings: the imputation of incomplete time series and the forecasting of upcoming hydrological conditions.  Results support the effectiveness of the transformer-based approach and highlight its capacity to capture and simulate the complex patterns present in hydrological data. In particular, the results indicate that diffusion models can efficiently sample realistic time series distributions under observation settings with variable missing data for both forecasting and imputation.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Ball-Codifference Screening for Heavy-Tailed Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20821)
*Mohsen Rezapour,Vahed Maroufy*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对高维数据中变量筛选时传统方法（协方差、相关、最小二乘）在预测变量稀疏或重尾分布下不稳定甚至失效的问题，基于扩展余差异和Ball-协方差思想，提出了Ball-余差异（Ball-codifference）统计量及其归一化筛选工具，构建了确定独立筛选程序。该统计量结合随机球的秩型几何与基于特征函数的余差异依赖度量，无需一阶或二阶矩存在。在大样本下通过有界V-统计量和泛函delta方法证明渐近正态性。模拟显示在重尾稳定分布下该方法能有效恢复高关联预测变量，实际数据示例表明该方法显著提升线性回归预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维筛选常用协方差、相关或最小二乘度量，但这些度量在预测变量稀疏或存在重尾分布时可能不稳定甚至无法定义。现有方法对矩条件要求较高，缺乏既能处理重尾性又能保持计算效率的筛选工具。

Method: 将随机球（Rank-type geometry of random balls）与余差异（基于特征函数的依赖度量）相结合，构建Ball-余差异统计量，并设计归一化筛选效用指标及确定独立筛选程序。理论证明采用有界V-统计量和泛函delta方法推导大样本正态性。

Result: 在高斯和亚高斯稳定分布模拟中，余差异加权的Ball筛选在恢复高关联预测变量方面表现优异，尤其在重尾程度显著时优势明显。实际数据应用表明该方法在线性回归中显著提升了预测精度。

Conclusion: Ball-余差异是一种无需有限一阶或二阶矩的高维变量筛选方法，能有效处理重尾数据，具有良好的理论性质和实证表现，是对现有基于协方差/相关筛选方法的重要补充。

Abstract: High-dimensional screening is commonly built on covariance, correlation, or least-squares measures. These summary measures can be unstable or even undefined, when predictors are sparse or have heavy-tailed distributions. Building on our recent work on extended codifference and the idea of Ball-covariance, we develop Ball-codifference for marginal screening in statistical modeling with heavy-tailed predictors and responses. The proposed statistic combines the rank-type geometry of random balls with the codifference as a dependency measure constructed based on the characteristic function, so it can be computed without requiring well-defined finite first or second moments. We define Ball-codifference and its normalized screening utility, and formulate a sure independence screening procedure. Large-sample normality follows from a bounded V-statistic and functional-delta-method argument under standard nondegeneracy and regularity conditions. Simulation studies under Gaussian and sub-Gaussian stable designs show that codifference-weighted Ball screening gives competitive or improved recovery of highly associated predictors, especially when tail heaviness is pronounced. Also, our data example illustrates that our variable screening method significantly improves prediction accuracy in linear regression.

</details>


### [238] [A Two-Sample Test on Weighted Persistence Intensity Functions in Topological Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20893)
*Yeongung Han,Ilmun Kim,Jisu Kim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 针对持久性图中强度函数的统计检验问题，提出了基于核的置换检验，证明了其在差异备择假设下的极小极大最优性，并通过带宽聚合框架实现实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图作为拓扑数据分析的核心工具，其强度函数的估计已有较多研究，但针对强度函数的统计假设检验（尤其是检验功效的理论性质）几乎空白。

Method: 提出基于核的置换检验；引入控制持久性图可能无界基数的假设条件，推导检验统计量的尖锐方差界；建立包含所有概率密度的概率模型；采用带宽聚合框架解决实际中最优带宽不可直接获取的问题。

Result: 证明了所提检验在备择假设下的极小极大最优性；推导了圆上Čech复形的持久性图的显式刻画；模拟与真实数据验证了方法的有效性和高经验功效。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了持久性图强度函数假设检验的理论空白，为TDA中的统计推断提供了具有最优理论保证且实际可行的检验方法。

Abstract: The intensity function, defined as the Lebesgue density of the expected measure of a persistence diagram, is a fundamental summary of the probability distribution of persistence diagrams in topological data analysis (TDA). Although several methods have been proposed for estimating intensity functions, statistical hypothesis testing for intensity functions remains largely unexplored. In particular, little is known about the power properties of hypothesis tests based on persistence diagrams.
  We propose a kernel-based permutation test and analyze its power against alternatives characterized by differences in persistence intensity functions. We introduce assumptions that control the effect of the possibly unbounded cardinality of persistence diagrams and yield a sharp variance bound for the test statistic. We also show that our probability model is broad enough to include all probability densities on the subset of $\mathbb{R}^2$ where $y>x\geq 0$. Using these results, we establish minimax optimality of the proposed test. Along the way, we derive an explicit characterization of the persistence diagram of the Čech complex on the circle.
  Since the optimal bandwidth is not directly accessible in practice, we adopt a bandwidth aggregation framework. Simulations and real-data applications demonstrate validity and high empirical power.

</details>


### [239] [Optimal use of a black-box learner in semiparametric estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21541)
*Yihong Gu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对部分线性模型，在结构无关设定下提出了一种新的目标线性系数估计器，其误差率达到1/√n + δ_{a,μ}·δ_{a,π} + [δ_s]²，并证明了该率不可改进。相比双机器学习（DML），该估计器消除了次优项max(δ_{a,μ}, δ_{a,π})·δ_s。在此基础上，进一步提出了一种通用方法TAME，通过对抗性条件矩校准局部编辑去偏权重，可严格改进DML保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双机器学习（DML）方法在处理部分线性模型时，当 nuisance 函数（μ₀和π₀）的估计难度不平衡时，其误差率包含次优项 max(δ_{a,μ}, δ_{a,π})·δ_s，导致效率损失。作者旨在消除这一次优项，实现更优且不可改进的估计率。

Method: 1) 提出了一种新型估计器，通过精心构造偏差校正项，使得误差率中 nuisance 估计误差以乘积形式 δ_{a,μ}·δ_{a,π} 出现，而非 max(δ_{a,μ}, δ_{a,π})·δ_s 形式。2) 在此基础上提出TAME（Transductive Adversarial Moment-calibrated Editing），一种通用框架，通过对抗性条件矩校准，在推断样本上局部编辑由黑箱回归估计诱导的去偏权重，可结合任意初始黑箱估计并严格改进DML。

Result: 建立了估计器的误差率 1/√n + δ_{a,μ}·δ_{a,π} + [δ_s]²，并给出了匹配下界证明该率不可改进。相比DML，消除了次优项 max(δ_{a,μ}, δ_{a,π})·δ_s。

Conclusion: 当 nuisance 函数估计难度不平衡时，TAME可严格改进DML保障。文中还讨论了如何充分利用TAME的优势，包括双学习器的增益、由此导出的模型选择欠平滑原则，以及对其他线性泛函估计问题的推广。

Abstract: Consider the partial linear model $Y = μ_0(X) + β_0 \cdot T + \varepsilon$ and $T = π_0(X) + u$ in the structure-agnostic setting, where we are blind to the structure $μ_0$ and $π_0$ and estimate the nuisances by a black-box hypothesis class. The learnability of the class is characterized by the estimation error $δ_s$ in the absence of model misspecification and its $L_2$ mis-specification error $δ_{a, μ}$ and $δ_{a, π}$ for $μ_0$ and $π_0$, respectively. We propose a novel estimator of the target linear coefficient $θ_0 = β_0$ with error rate \[
  \frac{1}{\sqrt{n}} + δ_{a, μ} \cdot δ_{a, π} + [δ_s]^2. \] A matching lower bound is also established, implying that this rate is unimprovable. Compared with the product rate yielded by double machine learning (DML), our estimator removes the suboptimal term $\max(δ_{a, μ}, δ_{a, π})\cdot δ_s$ at no extra cost or assumption.
  Building on the underlying insights, which are neither tailored to the one-learner setting nor the partial linear model, we propose Transductive Adversarial Moment-calibrated Editing (TAME), which locally edits debiasing weights induced by black-box regression estimates on the inference sample through adversarial conditional moment calibration. TAME can be combined with any initial black-box estimates and can strictly improve on DML guarantees when the nuisance difficulties are imbalanced. We discuss how to fully exploit the advantages introduced by TAME, including the gains from using two learners, the resulting under-smoothing principle for model selection, and extensions to other linear functional estimation problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Generative Bayesian Filtering for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20521)
*Lei Cao,Sihang Feng,Jixin Yan,Tao Sun,Naichen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式贝叶斯滤波（GBF）框架，用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）替代传统线性高斯观测模型，通过基于分数的后验采样实现状态估计，在合成数据和真实应用（制造系统监测、心律不齐诊断）中提升了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滤波方法（如卡尔曼滤波）依赖线性高斯等简单观测模型，无法刻画高维传感器信号中日益复杂非线性和异质模式。

Method: GBF框架：用预训练的CVAE作为条件生成模型替代传统观测模型；在线推理时通过贝叶斯预测-更新递归，将测量更新转化为后验采样问题；进一步转化为基于分数的采样问题，继承生成模型的灵活性和集成的不确定性量化能力。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实应用（制造系统监测、心律不齐诊断）上，GBF相比基线方法提升了状态估计的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GBF通过结合生成模型与贝叶斯滤波，有效处理非线性、高维、异质观测下的状态估计问题，兼具灵活性和不确定性量化优势。

Abstract: The state of a dynamic system evolves over time, switching among several latent modes that govern its observable behavior. Filtering methods infer the latent state from observations. Classical filtering approaches, including Kalman filters, typically rely on simple observation models, such as linear-Gaussian models, that are incapable of characterizing the increasingly nonlinear and heterogeneous patterns in high-dimensional sensor signals. To tackle the challenge, we propose Generative Bayesian Filtering (GBF), a filtering framework that replaces restrictive observation models with pretrained conditional generative models parametrized by conditional variational autoencoders (CVAE). For online inference, GBF performs a Bayesian prediction-update recursion in which the measurement update is formulated as a posterior sampling problem that combines the dynamical prior with the CVAE-induced likelihood. The resulting filtering problem is then transformed into a score-based sampling problem, which naturally inherits the flexibility from generative models and the uncertainty quantification capabilities from ensembling. Experiments on synthetic datasets and real-world applications involving manufacturing system monitoring and arrhythmia diagnosis demonstrate that GBF improves state estimation accuracy and robustness relative to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Heavy Log Curation: Perplexity-Based APT Detection via Unsupervised, Context-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20832)
*Shoya Otsu,Kei Suzuki,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAPTAIN利用预训练语言模型和困惑度评分，通过Q-Former桥接编码历史上下文，实现无需复杂预处理的APT日志检测，在降低开发成本的同时保持检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APT检测方法依赖大规模日志中极少量攻击事件，且需要精心标注的训练数据和复杂的预处理管道，工程成本高、难以规模化部署。

Method: 采用预训练语言模型进行困惑度评分，使用编码器模型+Q-Former桥接编码历史日志上下文，将紧凑上下文token注入解码器输入，并应用平滑滤波器稳定困惑度时间序列。

Result: 在APT基准测试上，CAPTAIN与强基线方法性能相当，且在输入数据经过较少预处理的情况下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CAPTAIN通过最小化领域特定预处理降低了日志检测系统的开发和运维成本，证明了通用预训练语言模型在APT检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) remain difficult to detect because only a small fraction of events in large-scale logs are attack-related, and investigation is expensive and hard to scale. Prior machine-learning approaches can reduce analyst workload, but they often rely on heavily curated training data and sophisticated preprocessing pipelines. Building and maintaining such pipelines require substantial domain expertise and engineering cost. Motivated by insights from a study of a strong APT detection baseline, we propose CAPTAIN (Context-Augmented Perplexity-based Threat Activity log detectIoN), a perplexity-based detector that leverages general, pre-trained language models with minimal, domain-agnostic preprocessing, enabling robust scoring of long, minimally processed log entries. CAPTAIN encodes recent history with an encoder model and a Q-Former-style bridge, then injects the compact context tokens into the decoder input so that perplexity reflects temporal context. To improve stability, CAPTAIN additionally applies smoothing filters to the perplexity time series. Across APT-oriented benchmarks, CAPTAIN competes with strong existing baselines and remains robust under substantially less curated inputs, that reduces the development and operational cost of advanced log preprocessing.

</details>


### [242] [CLOE: Christoffel Loss Autoencoder for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20530)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLOE方法，通过自编码器降维结合Christoffel函数进行半监督异常检测，利用新型损失函数引导表征学习以更好地捕捉正常数据分布的支持域，在多个高维表格异常检测基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Christoffel函数的异常检测方法在高维数据上扩展性差，而轻量级方法通常需要繁琐的多超参数调优。作者希望融合自编码器的降维能力与Christoffel函数的理论基础，实现既轻量（最多一个超参数）又能在高维场景下表现优异的半监督异常检测方法。

Method: CLOE设计包含三部分：1) 自编码器用于将高维数据降维到潜在空间；2) 在潜在空间中应用Christoffel函数进行异常检测；3) 新颖的损失函数利用Christoffel函数引导自编码器学习能更好表征正常数据分布支持域的表征。此外还提出基于理论的检测阈值设定流程和高效的单超参数调优策略。

Result: 在多个高维表格异常检测基准上，CLOE相比现有方法取得了更优的性能，同时保持了Christoffel函数方法的轻量级和低超参数调优优势。

Conclusion: 通过将Christoffel函数引入潜在空间并设计相应的损失函数进行表征学习引导，CLOE成功解决了Christoffel函数方法在高维场景下的扩展性问题，为半监督异常检测提供了一种既理论扎实又实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised anomaly detection plays a key role in diverse fields such as process monitoring, healthcare, and finance. However, lightweight methods often struggle with high-dimensional data and typically require careful tuning of multiple hyperparameters. Among existing approaches, Christoffel Function--based methods are attractive due to their simplicity, requiring at most a single hyperparameter. They also benefit from a well-established theoretical foundation that yields several interesting results for data science. However, their main limitation is poor scalability to high-dimensional settings. In this paper, we introduce CLOE, a new method that combines an autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a Christoffel Function--based detector applied in the latent space. To better align representation learning with anomaly detection, we design a novel loss function that leverages the Christoffel Function to guide the autoencoder toward representations that better capture the support of the normal data distribution. We further propose a principled procedure to set the detection threshold and an efficient strategy to tune the single remaining hyperparameter. Experiments on multiple high-dimensional tabular anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that CLOE achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, while preserving the lightweight and low-tuning advantages of Christoffel Function--based approaches.

</details>


### [243] [Agree on the Model, Verify the Inference: GKR Protocols for HND-Based Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21162)
*Xiaolong Liang,Juanjuan Li,Rui Qin,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GKR-HND是一种针对同态-非同态分解Transformer的注册模型验证协议，通过保留验证者检查GKR证明和注册权重，将昂贵的公共评估委托给计算工作者，在假设保留验证者诚实且证明者-工作者不共谋的条件下实现高效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包Transformer推理面临模型替换和不完整执行的风险，而直接重放又失去了委托计算的优势，需要在安全性和效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出GKR-HND协议：保留验证者检查GKR转录本和注册权重打开，将昂贵的公共评估委托给计算工作者；工作者返回签名的、绑定请求的响应，验证者仅当响应与证明声明一致时才接受。

Result: 在预训练HND模型上的实验验证了证明路径和委托公共计算的可行性，无需密集矩阵重放。

Conclusion: GKR-HND在安全性和效率之间取得了有效平衡，通过委托机制避免了完全重放的计算开销，同时通过注册模型和签名响应保证了推理完整性。

Abstract: Outsourced Transformer inference exposes clients to model substitution and incomplete execution, while direct replay removes the computational benefit of delegation. We present GKR-HND, a registered-model protocol for verifying the polynomial backbone of Homomorphic--Nonhomomorphic Decomposition Transformers. The retained verifier checks the GKR transcript and registered-weight openings, but delegates expensive public evaluations to an assigned computation worker. Assuming an honest retained verifier and prover--worker non-collusion, the verifier accepts only when the worker's signed, request-bound response agrees with the proof claims. Experiments with pretrained HND models validate the proof path and the delegated public computation without dense-matrix replay.

</details>


### [244] [Fisher Widths: Local Learning Geometry and Anisotropic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20578)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了统计流形上的高斯宽度复杂度，通过Fisher宽度和逆Fisher宽度这对泛函，分别刻画学习与恢复中的几何特性，并建立了二者之间的尖锐不等式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习中参数估计的局部几何结构由Fisher信息度量决定，而高维统计推断中的恢复问题则涉及逆Fisher度量。现有研究缺乏对这两种几何宽度及其相互关系的统一理解。

Method: 引入Fisher宽度和逆Fisher宽度，分别对应Fisher度量和逆Fisher度量诱导的高斯宽度；在Fisher正则损失下证明Fisher球上的尺度可达性；利用统计维数建立稀疏恢复的两侧估计，并推导两个宽度之间的乘积不等式。

Result: Fisher宽度在足够小的Fisher球上以scale达到；逆Fisher宽度揭示了各向异性高斯测量的几何效应，支持敏感恢复估计依赖于Fisher谱中活跃坐标的位置；两类宽度满足。

Conclusion: Fisher各向异性可以将复杂度从一种几何转移到另一种，但无法同时减小两种宽度相对于欧氏尺度的值。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-width complexity on statistical manifolds through a pair of functionals: the primal Fisher width $w_G(T) = w(G^{1/2}T)$, induced by the Fisher metric, and the inverse-Fisher width $w_{G^{-1}}(T) = w(G^{-1/2}T)$, induced by the inverse Fisher metric. The two widths play complementary statistical roles.
  On the learning side, the Fisher width measures the size of local parameter fluctuations in the geometry induced by the Fisher information. For Fisher-regular losses, we prove that the scale \(w_G(H_r)/\sqrt n\) is attained on sufficiently small Fisher balls.
  On the recovery side, the inverse-Fisher width captures the effect of anisotropic Gaussian measurements whose covariance is determined by the inverse Fisher information. For sparse recovery, the resulting geometry depends not only on sparsity but also on the position of the active coordinates in the Fisher spectrum. We obtain a two-sided estimate for the corresponding statistical dimension, together with support-sensitive recovery estimates and a natural ordering of supports with different curvature profiles.
  Finally, we establish a sharp relation between the primal and inverse-Fisher widths. On any common compact coordinate set $T$, they satisfy \[ w_G(T)w_{G^{-1}}(T)\geq w(T)^2. \] Thus, Fisher anisotropy may transfer complexity from one geometry to the other, but cannot reduce both widths relative to the Euclidean scale.

</details>


### [245] [When Does Recurrence Become an Algorithm? Convergence Selection in Weight-Tied Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20594)
*Tong Zhang,Junhao Hu,Yun Peng,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了权重共享的循环Transformer（单块重复T次）何时能实现真正的算法。通过在群词问题上的受控实验，发现了四条核心发现：训练预算定律、架构先验而非表达能力决定算法选择、计算复杂度壁垒与实际不符、机制可迁移但不可强制。并引入了收敛时间缩放因子τ(n,i)作为新的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解权重共享的循环Transformer是否以及如何学习实现真正的算法，而非仅仅拟合训练数据。现有标准评估工具在模型收敛到不动点时失效，需要新的分析方法和理论理解。

Method: 在群词问题上进行受控实验，使用权重共享的循环Transformer（单块重复T次）。通过训练预算定律分析计算速度与训练合约的关系，对比标准深度Transformer与权重共享Transformer的算法选择，研究NC1完全性与群阶数对学习的影响，以及通过热启动和输入调度测试机制的可迁移性。引入收敛时间缩放因子τ(n,i)作为因果分析工具。

Result: (1) 训练预算定律：v ~ n_train/T_train，指数0.98±0.04，R²=0.99；T=n时恰好为1。(2) 权重共享使算法选择从并行扫描转为串行前沿，即使提供了对数深度扫描的位置寻址。(3) NC1完全性不影响泛化（A5完全泛化），但群阶数造成障碍（S5的120×120算子导致联合学习死锁），操作优先课程可消除该壁垒。(4) 热启动可跨预算合约迁移算法并重新定价速度，但通过输入调度强制串行性失效。收敛时间缩放因子τ(n,i)可因果预测分布外行为。

Conclusion: 权重共享的循环Transformer确实能学习实现真正的算法，且其行为受训练预算、架构先验和问题结构的共同约束。标准评估工具因在不动点饱和而失效，新引入的收敛时间缩放因子提供了更有效的因果分析手段。这些发现对理解Transformer的计算能力和泛化机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: When does a weight-tied looped transformer -- one block applied T times -- implement an actual algorithm? We answer with four findings from controlled populations on group word problems. (1) The budget law: free training installs a linear computation frontier, a mechanism that solves v positions per loop, whose speed is priced by the training contract: v ~ n_train/T_train (exponent 0.98 +/- 0.04, R^2=0.99), exactly unity under T=n training. SGD selects a frontier matching the minimum the contract demands; granting more test-time loops than ever trained rescues late positions at fixed input length, yielding a principled halting rule T* = ceil(n / v-hat). (2) Architecture prior, not expressivity, picks the algorithm: standard-depth transformers learn parallel scans on this family; weight tying flips the selection to the serial frontier, even when positional addressing for a log-depth scan is supplied. At matched depth and parameters, untied models extrapolate worst and fail to learn A5 at all. (3) The walls are not where circuit complexity says: NC1-completeness costs nothing (A5 generalizes fully), while group order does (S5's 120x120 operator deadlocks joint learning) -- and an operator-first curriculum dissolves the wall in every seed. (4) Mechanisms are portable, not mandatable: warm-starting across budget contracts transfers the algorithm in every seed, re-pricing its speed, while imposing seriality through the input schedule fails where free training succeeds. These results are invisible to standard instruments, which provably saturate at the fixed points trained loops converge to. We introduce a head instrument, the convergence-time scaling tau(n,i), validate it causally via damage cones whose slope reproduces v, and show in-distribution head measurements predict out-of-distribution fate where tail metrics do not. Results replicate on the public easy-to-hard benchmark.

</details>


### [246] [Smooth Neural Point Processes via B-Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21098)
*Michele Bellomo,Riccardo Ramaschi,Alberto Dolara,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于B样条基函数的神经时间点过程模型，直接参数化条件强度函数，实现精确NLL计算、灵活网络架构、高效并行训练和光滑性正则化，在计算效率和预测精度上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经TPP模型在训练时需对补偿器建模、对网络架构施加约束，且事件贡献需顺序计算无法并行，导致计算开销大。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一种高效且灵活的神经TPP模型。

Method: 将条件强度函数（CIF）参数化为B样条基函数的非负组合，其系数由神经网络预测。该形式支持精确NLL计算、任意网络架构、训练并行化，并通过积分二阶导数实现CIF光滑性正则化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，所提方法相比参考神经TPP基线在计算效率和预测准确性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 直接参数化CIF的B样条方法能有效克服现有神经TPP模型的局限，兼顾表达力、计算效率与正则化能力。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) provide a general and flexible framework for modeling sequences of events in continuous time. Neural networks have been successfully employed to model TPPs in a highly expressive and data-driven way. Neural TPPs are typically trained via Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) by minimizing the negative log-likelihood (NLL), which depends on both the conditional intensity function (CIF) and its integral over time, the compensator. Recent neural TPP approaches enable exact evaluation of the NLL without numerical integration. However, these methods typically model the compensator rather than the CIF directly, impose constraints on the neural network architecture, and are computationally expensive during training, as event contributions to the NLL are evaluated sequentially rather than in parallel. In this work, we propose a novel neural TPP model that directly parametrizes the CIF as a non-negative combination of B-spline basis functions, whose coefficients are predicted by a neural network. This formulation enables exact evaluation of the NLL, preserves full flexibility in the neural architecture, allows efficient parallelization during training, and naturally supports CIF smoothness regularization through the integrated squared second derivative. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show improved computational efficiency and predictive accuracy compared to the reference neural TPP baseline.

</details>


### [247] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE框架，将网络压缩从离散域转换到连续函数希尔伯特空间，通过将单个神经元建模为秩1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，统一了剪枝和神经元合并作为低秩子空间投影，并引入宏块驱逐以统一度量涵盖多层结构（如残差通路），实现跨不同类型和尺寸层的无偏架构决策，是一个无数据、无超参数的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂表征，但解构其内部知识仍然具有挑战性。鉴于学习与压缩之间的联系，网络压缩为分析这些知识提供了有前景的视角，但标准压缩启发式方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差的影响。

Method: 提出HOPE框架：将网络压缩从离散域转换到希尔伯特连续函数空间；将神经元建模为秩1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，统一剪枝和神经元合并为低秩子空间投影；引入宏块驱逐（macro block eviction）以统一度量涵盖多层结构（如残差通路）。

Result: HOPE是一个无数据、无超参数的框架，通过模型压缩和微调的概念验证实验展示了该理论的实际潜力。

Conclusion: HOPE通过希尔伯特空间中的连续函数建模，实现了跨不同层类型和尺寸的无偏架构决策，为网络压缩提供了统一的数学框架，具有重要的理论价值和实践潜力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [248] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务语义通信中的协作可能产生有害影响的问题，提出一种基于语义感知的任务聚类方法。该方法先进行短期初始训练，再利用层次化密度聚类将语义一致的任务分组成簇，最后在簇内进行端到端联合训练。仿真表明该方法能有效消除有害协作和负迁移，在分类准确率上优于无聚类多任务和单任务训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协同多任务语义通信（CMT-SemCom）中，任务之间的语义关系既可能导致建设性协作也可能导致破坏性协作，现有工作[1]已证明了这一点。为确保协作始终是建设性的，需要一种能够根据任务语义关系进行合理聚类的方法。

Method: 提出一个序列化多阶段优化框架：(i) 先进行短期初始训练，然后利用层次化基于密度的空间聚类（HDBSCAN）对语义对齐的任务进行聚类；(ii) 在已发现的任务簇内进行端到端（E2E）联合训练，不同簇之间不共享参数。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架能够有效缓解破坏性协作和负迁移问题，在分类准确率上相比未聚类的多任务学习和单任务学习基线均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的任务聚类，可以将语义一致的任务分组训练，避免不相干任务之间的相互干扰，从而在CMT-SemCom中实现建设性的多任务协作。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [249] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用有限样本保证高召回流水线中未被召回的相关项数量很小，证明仅从候选集内部标注无法认证遗漏质量，必须对外部排除池进行采样；给出了匹配的有限样本下界，并开发了一套基于二项和超几何分布的精确有限样本工具包，用于认证遗漏质量、召回率、多候选生成器联合认证及压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高召回流水线（如实证研究中的初始高召回阶段）中，若相关项未被召回，后续所有阶段都将丢失这些项。因此需要一种方法，用有限样本保证被遗漏的相关项质量很小，且认证结论具有有限样本有效性。

Method: 首先理论分析证明：仅标注候选集内部无法认证任何非平凡遗漏质量上界，必须采样排除池；然后证明匹配的有限样本下界，说明任何有效审计必须检查约N0/m个排除池标签。在此基础上，利用二项分布和超几何分布反演（而非渐近近似）开发精确有限样本工具包，支持遗漏质量认证、双池设计下召回率转换、预指定嵌套候选生成器族的同时认证以及压力测试认证。

Result: 证明了排除池审计是遗漏质量认证在零遗漏场景下的极小极大速率最优策略；开发了精确有限样本工具包，所有保证均在候选生成器（或预指定族）和审计规则在查看认证标签前固定的条件下成立。

Conclusion: 排除池审计不仅是方便的，更是理论上最优的遗漏质量认证方法；所提出的精确有限样本工具包可与可观测的审查负担配对，用以选择满足遗漏质量目标的最小负担候选生成器。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime.
