This phenomenon is identified and formalized as Spectral Drift -- the frequency-domain distance between consecutive layer activations -- with empirical validation showing that failures exhibit significantly higher drift than correct predictions (1.9% increase, p<0.001). This spectral signature emerges during internal processing but becomes masked in final outputs, explaining why confidence-based detection methods struggle.
  This work introduces Self-Detecting Neural Networks (SDNN), a framework that monitors spectral dynamics across network depth using Short-Time Fourier Transform, wavelet decomposition, and statistical moments to capture multi-scale spectral features. A lightweight detector network (5% parameter overhead) learns to identify failure-indicative patterns via curriculum learning on progressively challenging distributions: natural misclassifications, distribution shifts, and adversarial perturbations.
  Experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate that SDNN achieves 79.0 +/- 25.3% AUROC across three seeds, substantially outperforming confidence-based baselines including MaxSoftmax (50.5%) and Energy Score (52.9%) by approximately 25-30 percentage points. Ablation studies reveal that wavelet decomposition and statistical features make consistent contributions, while STFT's role remains unclear. This work establishes spectral analysis of internal activations as a promising direction for neural network reliability, revealing diagnostic information inaccessible to output-based approaches.

</details>


### [285] [STeMP: Spatio-Temporal Modelling Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20592)
*Jan Linnenbrink,Jakub Nowosad,Marvin Ludwig,Anna Frederike Jablotschkin,Fabian Schumacher,Teja Kattenborn,Hanna Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时空机器学习建模缺乏标准化报告协议的问题，本文提出了STeMP（时空建模协议），该协议包含概述、模型和预测三个部分，并提供了R包和GitHub托管，旨在实现标准化报告、指导建模过程，并通过警告机制帮助作者和审稿人识别常见陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空机器学习模型对训练数据特征和方法选择高度敏感，但当前缺乏用于时空建模的标准化报告协议，导致模型的可信度、透明度和可比性不足。

Method: 设计了STeMP协议，包含三个模块（概述、模型、预测）；开发了R包和Web应用程序，支持手动或半自动填写协议；通过GitHub托管协议定义并鼓励社区贡献；协议内嵌了常见陷阱的警告机制。

Result: 提出了STeMP协议框架及配套的R包和Web应用，实现了标准化报告功能和建模过程指导；协议可基于建模对象半自动填写，并能返回警告以识别常见问题。

Conclusion: STeMP填补了时空机器学习标准化报告协议的空白，有助于提升模型在科学和实践应用中的可信度、透明度与可比较性；通过开源社区协作可进一步完善该协议。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal machine-learning modelling is an important tool in environmental research. However, machine-learning models are highly sensitive to both the characteristics of the training data, such as its distribution, and methodological choices, including the cross-validation strategy. Each decision has impact and implications on the model itself as well as the estimation of the model quality and applicability for certain purposes. Taking into account the large role of machine-learning based maps of the environment in science and their transfer into practice, transparent reporting of spatio-temporal models, ideally using standardized model protocols, is essential to enable trust, transparency and comparability. However, such protocols are currently lacking for spatio-temporal modelling.
  We propose STeMP (Spatio-Temporal Modelling Protocol) to fill this gap by serving two purposes: standardized reporting to understand the model functioning as well as providing guidance during the modelling process by pointing at critical decisions and parameters. The protocol is structured in three sections: Overview, Model and Prediction. The Overview section contains metadata, while the Model and Prediction sections go into detail, describing predictors, evaluation and software, and further relevant elements of the modelling workflow.
  The protocol definition is hosted on GitHub and accompanied by an R-package (https://github.com/LOEK-RS/STeMP). The R-package contains a web application that can be used to fill the protocol either manually or in a semi-automated way from provided modelling objects. Warnings are returned from the protocol when common pitfalls are encountered, which may help authors as a guide through the modelling process but also support reviewers in the assessment of modelling studies. Via GitHub, incorporation of contributions and feedback from the community is encouraged.

</details>


### [286] [Are Single-Token Sparse Autoencoder Features Causally Necessary? Layer-Depth and SAE-Family Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20596)
*Seonglae Cho,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Rishi Kalra,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对6个模型和3个SAE家族的390万个特征进行零消融分析，发现单token特征在不同SAE家族间的因果作用不一致，跨家族的因果差异大于家族内的规模效应，且训练方法（而非激活函数或规模）是导致可解释性声明敏感性的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征被广泛用于解释和引导大语言模型，但尚未检验这些特征的因果作用在不同SAE家族间是否保持稳定。单token特征提供了具有真实标签的基准案例，使得直接比较成为可能。

Method: 分析了6个模型和3个SAE家族（GemmaScope, BatchTopK, LlamaScope）的390万个特征，在全层深度进行零消融实验。通过单token特征在解码器空间的聚类程度、层分布、消融后的logit变化等指标进行因果稳定性评估。

Result: 单token特征在解码器空间中聚类紧密程度高出4.7倍，集中在早期层。消融后在208个全层条件中有178个产生显著logit降低。跨家族因果差异大于家族内规模效应：GemmaScope和BatchTopK特征保持因果锚定，而LlamaScope特征局部冗余。目标token秩在消融后96-98%恢复到基线2倍以内。受控激活函数比较在同一模型内出现符号反转。

Conclusion: 跨家族的可解释性声明对训练方法敏感，而非仅受激活函数或规模影响。训练方法是导致SAE特征因果稳定性差异的主要残差因素。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are used to interpret and steer large language models, yet whether a feature's causal role is stable across SAE families remains untested. Single-token features that activate on one vocabulary item provide the diagnostic case where ground truth permits direct comparison. We analyze 3.9M features across six models and three SAE families using zero-ablation at full layer depth. Single-token features cluster 4.7x tighter in decoder space and concentrate in early layers (Layer 0 in GPT2-Small; L0-L4 in Gemma). Ablating them yields Benjamini-Hochberg-significant logit reductions in 178 of 208 full-layer conditions, with depth controlling whether damage cascades downstream or shapes the output directly. Cross-family causal differences exceed within-family scale effects: on the same base model, GemmaScope and BatchTopK features remain causally anchored, while LlamaScope features are locally redundant. The target token's rank recovers to within 2x baseline 96-98% of the time after the same ablation, and a controlled activation-function comparison reverses sign within the same model, leaving training recipe as the residual candidate. Cross-family interpretability claims are therefore sensitive to training methodology, not just activation function or scale.

</details>


### [287] [One Round Is All You Need: Analytic Federated Learning for Task-Heterogeneous Multi-Label Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20641)
*Afsaneh Mahanipour,Hana Khamfroush*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向任务异构场景的解析式联邦学习框架，通过三次闭式运算替代迭代优化，在多标签医学图像分类中仅需两轮通信即可显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法在任务异构（各机构仅标注部分疾病类别）下存在两个问题：需要数百轮通信才能收敛，且缺失类别标签引入系统性假阴性偏差无法校正。

Method: 提出解析式联邦学习框架，包含三个闭式操作：1）平衡标签投影，归一化正负贡献消除类别不平衡偏差；2）每类绝对聚合规则，独立组装各类别最优岭回归分类器；3）可选的解析伪标签精炼轮次，通过置信度过滤的教师分类器向非标注客户端传播缺失类别知识。

Result: 在ChestXray14上四种逐步加重的缺失类别配置下，所提方法持续超越SOTA联邦多标签方法FedMLP，最高提升18.44 BACC点和13.24 AUC点，同时大幅降低通信开销。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需至多两轮通信，与任务异构程度或客户端数量无关，为医疗图像多标签分类在异构标注场景下提供了高效且有效的联邦学习方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple clinical institutions to collaboratively train a shared disease classifier without centralizing patient data. In practice, however, each institution annotates only the pathologies within its area of expertise, so the federation operates under task heterogeneity: each client holds labels for a strict subset of the target disease categories while the remaining classes are entirely unobserved at that site. Existing gradient-based FL methods fail under this setting because they require hundreds of communication rounds to converge and because missing class labels introduce systematic false-negative bias that the model cannot correct without a principled mechanism. We propose an analytic federated learning framework for multi-label medical image classification under task heterogeneity. The proposed method replaces iterative gradient optimization with three closed-form operations: a balanced label projection that neutralizes class-imbalance bias by normalizing positive and negative contributions to equal total mass; a per-class absolute aggregation law that independently assembles the optimal ridge-regression classifier for each disease category from the sufficient statistics uploaded by its annotating clients; and an optional analytic pseudo-label refinement round that propagates missing-class knowledge from a confidence-filtered teacher classifier to non-annotating clients. The entire procedure requires at most two communication rounds, irrespective of the degree of task heterogeneity or the number of participating clients. Experiments on ChestXray14 under four progressively severe missing-class configurations demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art federated multi-label method FedMLP by up to 18.44 BACC points and 13.24 AUC points, while reducing the communication.

</details>


### [288] [Scaling Interpretable Transformers with Parity Bottleneck Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20652)
*Andrew Mack,Kraig Yuheng Tou,Mark Henry,Zhengxun Wu,Lauren Greenspan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ParityTransformer，通过在每层引入Deep Parity Bottleneck（DPB）——一种参数无关的代数字典结构，实现高效、宽且稀疏的中间表示，解决了可解释性瓶颈在大规模模型中的计算和内存开销问题。实验表明，该方法在后验稀疏自编码器任务上表现相当或更优，且特征原生参与前向计算，朝向设计可解释模型迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型存在超位置现象，后验稀疏自编码器（SAEs）虽能恢复特征，但训练本身可解释的模型因逐层过完备瓶颈的内存和计算代价过高而难以实现。

Method: 提出Deep Parity Bottleneck（DPB），用参数无关的代数字典替代学习得到的过完备基，提供确定性去相关保证；采用分层结构的稀疏瓶颈，结合多级混合专家方法实现硬件感知的高效稀疏训练，缩小激活稀疏与密集训练之间的成本差距。

Result: ParityTransformer在稀疏探测任务上与后验SAEs表现相当或更优，在特征吸收、引导有效性和细粒度因果干预等指标上领先。

Conclusion: ParityTransformer的特征原生参与模型前向计算，解决了SAEs探测的特征是否真正被模型使用的问题，是朝向设计可解释模型而非后验恢复的重要步骤。

Abstract: Language models are thought to exhibit the phenomenon of superposition, representing many more features than dimensions in their residual streams. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are designed to recover such features post-hoc, but training models that are interpretable by construction has remained impractical, as a per-layer over-complete bottleneck is prohibitively expensive in both memory and compute. To overcome this issue, we introduce the ParityTransformer, a GPT-2-scale architecture whose intermediate representations are efficient and wide / sparse by design. At each layer, a Deep Parity Bottleneck (DPB) replaces a learned over-complete basis with a parameter-free algebraic dictionary, providing a deterministic incoherence guarantee and eliminating the memory requirements that have prevented per-layer interpretable bottlenecks at scale. A DPB is a hierarchically structured sparse bottleneck which efficiently enforces sparsity using a multi-level mixture-of-experts approach: a hardware-aware implementation that closes the cost gap between activation sparse and dense training to a manageable interpretability tax. Empirically, ParityTransformers perform at least as well as post-hoc SAEs on sparse probing tasks, while out-performing on measures of feature absorption, steering effectiveness, and fine-grained causal interventions. Because subsequent computation acts only on features that survive the sparse bottleneck, the ParityTransformer's features are native to the model's forwards pass by construction, addressing the question of whether SAEs probe features the model actually uses during computation. We see this as a step toward training models whose internal representations are interpretable by design rather than recovered post hoc.

</details>


### [289] [SalesLoop: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Sales Lead Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20655)
*Chenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SalesLoop是一个基于强化学习的CRM线索排序框架，通过性能感知奖励和判别式GRPO优化目标，解决了离线高精度模型在线下效果差的问题。在160天生产A/B测试中，NDCG@K提升7.9%，P@K提升15.8%，新能车企实验显示累计提升4.7%-8.7%，Top-10%召回率达44.1%，高意图线索转化率是人工基线的2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRM系统中的线索排序面临离线模型高精度但在线效果差的持续挑战，根本原因在于三个关键脱节：离线与在线指标不匹配、逐点与列表目标不一致、时间分布漂移。

Method: 提出SalesLoop强化学习框架：(1) 性能感知奖励函数，编码基于排序位置和转化速度加权的转化结果；(2) 判别式GRPO，将Group Relative Policy Optimization适配到判别式排序模型的列表优化目标，建立模型预测与真实业务结果的闭环反馈。

Result: NDCG@K提升7.9%，P@K提升15.8%。160天某新能车企A/B测试（1650万线索、280名销售）显示累计提升4.7%(p=0.047)和8.7%(p=0.002)。Top-10%召回率44.1%，高意图线索转化率为销售基线2.3倍。

Conclusion: SalesLoop通过强化学习闭环有效弥合了离线与在线的性能差距，在实际业务场景中取得了统计显著的业务指标提升，验证了方法在CRM线索排序中的实用价值。

Abstract: Lead ranking in Customer Relationship Management (CRM) systems faces a persistent challenge: models achieving high offline accuracy often underperform in production. We identify three fundamental gaps responsible for this disconnect: offline-online metric mismatch, pointwise-listwise objective misalignment, and temporal distribution drift. To address these gaps, we propose SalesLoop, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a closed feedback loop between model predictions and real-world business outcomes. Our approach introduces (1) a performance-aware reward that encodes conversion outcomes weighted by ranking position and conversion velocity, and (2) Discriminative GRPO, a listwise optimization objective that adapts Group Relative Policy Optimization to discriminative ranking models.
  SalesLoop improves NDCG@K by +7.9\% and P@K by +15.8\% over the strongest static baseline. A 160-day production A/B test at a New Energy Vehicle manufacturer, spanning 16.5M leads and 280 sales specialists across two provincial markets, validates statistically significant cumulative lift of +4.7\% ($p=0.047$) and +8.7\% ($p=0.002$). In production, the ranking backbone achieves Top-10\% recall of 44.1\% and surfaces high-intent leads at $2.3\times$ the conversion rate of specialist baselines.

</details>


### [290] [Adaptive Multi-Horizon Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20656)
*Manoosh Samiei,Doina Precup,Paul Masset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多时域自适应折扣方法，能够在强化学习中动态选择并组合多个时间视野，无需手动调节折扣因子，在MiniGrid环境（含持续学习场景）中验证了其有效性，展现出更好的参数效率和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定折扣因子在复杂动态环境中难以兼顾短期与长期回报，而生物智能体表现出灵活的自适应时间折扣，因此需要一种能自动调整多个时间尺度的强化学习方法。

Method: 提出多时域（multi-horizon）方法，自适应地选择并组合多个时间视野（temporal horizons），无需人工调参，适用于任务切换和环境配置变化的持续学习场景。

Result: 在MiniGrid环境（包括三个顺序变化任务的持续学习场景）中，该方法能够自动识别有效的折扣因子，表现出鲁棒的适应性。

Conclusion: 自适应时间折扣能够提升参数效率，增强人工和类脑学习系统在动态环境中的适应能力。

Abstract: Effective decision-making in complex and changing environments requires balancing short-term and long-term consequences. In reinforcement learning (RL), this trade-off is typically controlled through a fixed discount factor, which imposes a single exponentially discounted temporal horizon. However, biological agents exhibit flexible and adaptive temporal discounting, suggesting that effective planning requires multiple timescales. Here, we propose a multi-horizon approach that adaptively selects and combines temporal horizons, enabling robust adaptation to changes in reward structure without manual discount-factor tuning. This flexibility makes the method particularly suitable for continual learning scenarios involving task switches and varying environmental configurations. Empirically, we demonstrate that our approach identifies effective discount factors across a range of MiniGrid environments, including continual settings composed of three sequentially changing tasks. These results suggest that adaptive temporal discounting can improve parameter efficiency and enhance adaptability in both artificial and biologically inspired learning systems.

</details>


### [291] [End-to-End Learning of Safe Optimal Feedback Control in High Dimensions with Control Barrier Function Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20674)
*Xingjian Li,Kelvin Kan,Deepanshu Verma,Krishna Kumar,Stanley Osher,Samy Wu Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合算子分裂与Jacobian-Free反向传播（JFB）方法，将基于二次规划的安全滤波器嵌入端到端策略训练，实现了在状态维度高达1200、控制维度高达400的高维多智能体非线性控制问题中，保持硬安全约束的CBF安全滤波器可扩展训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CBF的端到端策略训练方法因嵌入QP安全滤波器带来的计算和微分瓶颈，只能处理低维系统（通常≤16维），缺乏可扩展至高维系统的解决方案。

Method: 将算子分裂技术与Jacobian-Free Backpropagation（JFB）方法结合，使QP安全滤波器层可微且可扩展；利用非光滑分析理论为训练方法提供理论依据。

Result: 在状态维度1200、控制维度400的高维多智能体非线性控制问题上验证了有效性，突破了原有16维的限制。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持CBF硬安全保证的同时，实现了端到端训练的可扩展性，适用于高维系统。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning high-dimensional semi-global feedback controllers under hard safety constraints enforced by control barrier functions (CBFs). Incorporating CBFs into end-to-end policy training requires embedding a quadratic-program-based safety filter as an optimization layer, but computational and differentiation bottlenecks have largely restricted prior approaches to low-dimensional systems, typically with at most 16 state dimensions. We address this limitation by combining operator splitting with the recently developed Jacobian-Free Backpropagation (JFB) method to enable scalable end-to-end training while preserving hard safety guarantees through the CBF safety filter. We justify this training methodology theoretically using nonsmooth analysis techniques and demonstrate its effectiveness on high-dimensional multi-agent nonlinear control problems with state and control dimensions up to 1200 and 400, respectively.

</details>


### [292] [Explanation-Based Runtime Verification for Trustworthy ML-driven Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20675)
*Omran Ayoub,Carlos Natalino,Ali Al Housseini,Felix Foschum,Philipp Morger,Tiziano Leidi,David Hock,Paolo Monti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解释的运行时验证方法，利用模型解释（如特征重要性、交互分析）在部署前评估ML决策的合理性，通过检查解释的一致性和物理合理性来拦截错误决策，并在光路传输质量分类任务中验证了有效性，能在保持高自动化率的同时拦截大量错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML模型在光网络自动化中承担关键决策任务（如故障管理、资源分配），错误决策会直接影响服务质量、资源效率和网络稳定性。现有XAI技术虽能提升透明度，但缺乏在部署时主动验证决策可靠性的机制，亟需一种能在控制环路执行前评估单次ML决策合理性的方法。

Method: 提出基于解释的运行时验证方法，核心思路是在ML决策执行前，利用模型解释（如特征重要性、特征交互分析）对决策进行双重检验：(1) 解释一致性评估——判断当前预测的解释是否与模型在类似输入上的行为一致；(2) 物理基础一致性评估——判断解释是否符合领域物理规律。对未通过验证的决策进行延迟执行或拒绝。

Result: 在光路传输质量分类任务上进行了实验验证，结果表明该方法能够在保持高自动化率的前提下，拦截显著比例的错误决策。

Conclusion: 基于解释的运行时验证是一种有效的ML决策可靠性保障手段，能够在不显著降低自动化水平的前提下提高网络控制环路的安全性。该方法为高可靠性要求的网络自动化系统提供了新的可信保障思路。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models are increasingly integrated into optical network automation frameworks to support tasks such as failure management, performance monitoring and resource allocation. In these environments, ML-driven predictions may be directly coupled with control-plane actions where incorrect decisions can immediately impact service quality, resource efficiency, and network stability. As automation levels increase, ensuring the reliability of individual decisions at deployment time becomes a critical requirement. Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques have emerged to improve transparency by highlighting the factors influencing ML predictions. In addition to identifying influential features, they provide insights into the underlying reasoning process of the model, revealing how different input variables contribute to the final outcome and how feature interactions shape the decision boundary. In this work, we introduce explanation-based runtime verification, an approach that exploits model explanations to assess the soundness of individual ML decisions before they are executed in the network control loop. The proposed approach evaluates explanation coherence and physics grounding consistency at runtime, enabling the system to defer or reject decisions flagged as uncertain. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a representative use case of lightpath quality of transmission classification. Experimental results show that explanation-based verification can intercept a significant fraction of erroneous decisions while preserving high automation rate.

</details>


### [293] [Attribution Markets: A Fisher-Market Formulation for Fractional Credit Assignment Between Planned Tasks and Performed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20694)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用拟线性Fisher市场（计划任务为买方，已执行动作为可分商品）来桥接个人/组织规划系统中计划与执行之间的记录鸿沟，并引入熵正则化推广方案以增强对噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统使用排他性的全有或全无链接来关联计划任务（预算）与已执行动作（时长/描述），导致真正相关但未链接的努力被孤立，且活跃目标上误报停滞。需要一种更灵活的桥接机制。

Method: 将桥接问题建模为拟线性Fisher市场：计划任务是预算约束的买家，已执行动作是可分商品，融合文本/结构/时间信号设定买家估值。引入保留价格和现金选项实现守恒、硬预算上限和垃圾过滤定理。扩展市场以包含凹完成效用贴现（任务接近计划时折扣进度），通过饱和阈值不动点（Brouwer）保证存在性，对角占优条件保证局部唯一性。进一步提出单参数熵正则化泛化方案及噪声自适应规则。

Result: 在随机和对抗性实例上验证了不动点理论的有效性；发现市场尖锐的零熵均衡对亲和噪声比熵正则化最优传输的永久平滑方案更敏感；提出的熵正则化泛化方案统一了二者，噪声自适应规则改善了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 拟线性Fisher市场为计划-执行桥接提供了可理论保证的框架，熵正则化推广提升了噪声鲁棒性，结果与多触点归因、最优传输和在线Fisher市场算法相关。

Abstract: Personal and organizational planning systems maintain two records that drift apart: what was planned (a task's effort budget) and what was done (a logged action's duration and description). Existing systems bridge them with an exclusive, all-or-nothing link that strands genuinely related but unlinked effort and reports false stalls on active goals. We formulate the bridge as a quasi-linear Fisher market: planned tasks are budget-constrained buyers, performed actions are divisible goods, and a fused text/structural/temporal signal sets each buyer's valuation. Two market instruments - a seller reserve price and a buyer cash option - yield conservation, a hard budget cap, and a provable junk filter as theorems. We extend the market with a concave completion utility discounting progress as a task nears its plan; standard convergence theory for the market's algorithm does not transfer here, resolved by a satiation-threshold fixed point with existence (Brouwer) and local uniqueness under an explicit diagonal-dominance condition, validated empirically on random and adversarial instances. A de-circularized, multi-seed benchmark - observed affinity corrupted independently of the scored ground truth - surfaces a genuine weak spot: the market's sharp, zero-entropy equilibrium is more sensitive to affinity noise than entropy-regularized optimal transport's permanently smoothed one. We resolve this with a one-parameter entropy-regularized generalization unifying the two, plus a noise-adaptive rule for its regularization strength. We report full reproducibility parameters, discuss limitations candidly, and relate the result to multi-touch attribution, optimal transport, and online Fisher-market algorithms.

</details>


### [294] [CEDAR: Causal Edge Discovery for Autoregressive Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20696)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CEDAR是一种基于约束的稀疏自回归时间序列滞后因果边发现方法，通过AR(1)残差化距离相关筛选、条件独立性检验和稳定MCI剪枝，在稀疏场景下仅需O(d²)次检验即可完成因果发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理稀疏自回归时间序列的滞后因果发现时，要么计算复杂度高，要么在数据稀缺时表现不佳，亟需一种在数据有限条件下高效且可解释的方法。

Method: (1) 使用AR(1)残差化的U中心距离相关筛选候选跨变量滞后；(2) 对每个显著的跨变量滞后候选执行两次有目标的条件独立性检验，每个有序对最多接受一个滞后；(3) 通过稳定MCI剪枝步骤移除间接边；(4) 可选确定性C节点调整趋势型非平稳性。

Result: 在稀疏场景下（仅有少数滞后通过筛选），CEDAR在筛选后仅需O(d²)次条件独立性检验，同时保持了边级别的可解释性。

Conclusion: CEDAR在数据稀缺且变量具有一阶自回归动力学时效果最佳；而当时间序列长度T增大或存在高阶自回归/多滞后同步效应时，使用更丰富条件集的方**更优。

Abstract: We propose CEDAR (Causal Edge Discovery for Autoregressive Processes), a constraint-based method for lagged causal edge discovery in sparse autoregressive time series. CEDAR screens candidate cross-variable lags using AR(1)-residualized, U-centered distance correlation, then applies two targeted conditional-independence tests per significant cross-variable lag candidate and accepts at most one lag per ordered pair. A stable MCI pruning step removes indirect edges, and optional deterministic C-nodes adjust for specified trend-like nonstationarity. In sparse regimes where few lags survive screening, CEDAR requires $O(d^2)$ CI tests after screening while retaining edge-level interpretability. CEDAR is most effective when data are scarce and variables exhibit lag-1 self-dynamics; methods with richer conditioning sets become preferable as $T$ grows or when higher-order autoregressive or simultaneous multi-lag effects are common.

</details>


### [295] [Perspective Latents as an Architectural Condition for Causal Emergence in Active Inference Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20708)
*Hongju Pae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究主动推理智能体中集成信息分解（Φᵣ）的因果涌现特性，发现Φᵣ主要集中于慢速全局潜变量g，其聚合规模主要源于架构且随训练递减，仅在原子组成层面（解耦符号翻转、向下因果承载）才能揭示学习效应，表明标量Φᵣ不能直接反映学习整合程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现强化学习智能体中Φᵣ随训练增长并追踪奖励改善，但对于无奖励的主动推理预测性组织如何与这类信息论特征关联尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建一个具有快慢双潜变量（快速感知z、慢速全局g）分离架构的主动推理智能体，其中g由预测误差驱动且与策略梯度结构解耦，在无奖励的环境切换协议下进行测试。

Result: Φᵣ集中在g中；聚合Φᵣ大小主要来自架构因素，随训练递减；学习效应仅在原子组成层面显现：解耦从负变为正符号且对环境变化保持不变，向下因果承载了环境依赖的调节。

Conclusion: g是主动推理智能体中Φᵣ相关时间组织的架构位点，标量Φᵣ不能直接作为学习整合的索引。

Abstract: A recent line of work measures causal emergence in reinforcement learning agents through Integrated Information Decomposition, reporting that $Φ_r$ grows with training and tracks reward improvement. For active inference, this raises the question of how reward-free predictive organization relates to such information-theoretic signatures. I test this within an active inference agent whose architecture separates a fast perception latent $z$ from a slow global latent $g$, where $g$ is driven by prediction error and structurally decoupled from policy gradients. In a reward-free environmental regime-switching protocol, $Φ_r$ concentrates in $g$; its aggregate magnitude is largely architectural and decreases with training. The substantive effect of learning becomes legible only at the atom-compositional level: decoupling flips sign from negative to positive and becomes regime-invariant under environmental change, while downward causation carries the regime-dependent adjustment. These results identify $g$ as the architectural locus of $Φ_r$-relevant temporal organization in an active inference agent, and argue against reading scalar $Φ_r$ as a direct index of learned integration.

</details>


### [296] [LLMs Get Lost in Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20734)
*Jihoon Tack,Philippe Laban,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将静态单轮任务转化为动态多轮对话的框架，用于评估LLM在用户意图随对话演变时的跟踪和行动能力。研究发现，在静态场景中表现优秀的模型在动态意图演变场景中性能大幅下降，表明当前LLM尚未具备忠实跟踪和响应不断变化的用户意图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM主要在单轮、完全指定的设置下评估或训练，但真实交互中用户的意图是动态变化的（逐步透露、修改、重定向），因此需要探究LLM在对话过程中跟踪和响应演变中用户意图的能力。

Method: 提出一个框架，将静态单轮任务转换为动态多轮对话，其中用户意图在轮次间逐步演变（逐步透露、修改、中途重定向），同时保留原始任务的评估协议，使现有基准可重复用作受控测试平台而无需新标注。

Result: 在多个任务上发现一致现象：强静态设置性能无法迁移到动态意图演变设置中，模型家族普遍出现显著性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前LLM未能忠实跟踪和响应用户演变的意图，这一能力对未来的协作代理至关重要，但在静态评估中不可见，揭示了评估范式的根本性差距。

Abstract: As LLMs become more capable, they are increasingly deployed as collaborative agents, taking on user-delegated tasks through iterative interaction. Yet genuine interaction is inherently dynamic: users rarely specify their intent upfront, instead disclosing, revising, and reshaping it as the conversation unfolds. Despite this, LLMs are still predominantly evaluated or trained in single-turn, fully-specified settings, leaving open a fundamental question: how well do LLMs track and act on user intent as it evolves over the course of a conversation? To study this, we introduce a framework that transforms static, single-turn tasks into dynamic multi-turn conversations in which the user's intent evolves across turns--incrementally revealed, revised, and at times redirected mid-conversation--while preserving each task's original evaluation protocol, enabling existing benchmarks to be reused as controlled testbeds without new annotation. Across multiple tasks, we surface a consistent phenomenon: strong static-setting performance does not transfer to the evolving-intent setting, with substantial drops across model families. Our findings point to a fundamental gap: today's LLMs do not yet faithfully track and act on the user's evolving intent, a capability invisible to static evaluation yet critical for future collaborative agents.

</details>


### [297] [Cardinality-Decomposed Loss: Matching Training Objectives to Relation Structure in Heterogeneous Recommendation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20737)
*Parul Maheshwari,Amulya Paruchuri,Yiqing Zou,Alireza Sahami Shirazi,Farhad Farahani,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推荐系统中异质二分图常用的BPR损失会导致属性嵌入坍塌为近乎随机的几何结构（静默失效），并提出结合交叉熵与BPR的基数分解损失（CDL）来解决该问题，通过λ参数平衡排序与属性判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统在异质二分图上对所有关系使用统一的BPR损失，虽然对推荐任务有效，但会导致属性嵌入退化为接近随机的几何结构，这种静默失效不被标准排序指标察觉，却污染用户节点嵌入，损害下游任务。

Method: 提出基数分解损失（CDL），结合交叉熵（CE）和BPR损失，分别处理不同基数关系（一对多与一对一）；引入λ参数权衡排序性能与属性判别性；通过语义对齐和拓扑泄漏两个图属性解释数据集行为。

Result: 在5个数据集上验证CDL一致提升了属性嵌入的可判别性；NDCG在属性携带有效偏好信号时提升，在相关性弱时与之冲突；λ扫描揭示数据集行为由语义对齐和拓扑泄漏两个图属性决定。

Conclusion: CDL有效解决了统一损失函数（BPR）导致属性嵌入坍塌的问题，通过基数感知的损失组合和阈值权衡，在保持推荐性能的同时提升属性嵌入质量，为异质图推荐系统的损失设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks trained on heterogenous bipartite graphs form a common basis in recommendation systems. These graphs often express relations that vary in cardinality, for example, user-item preferences are one-to-many and user-attribute features are one-to-one. Traditionally, a unique loss function is applied for all of the network components which is often Bayesian Personalized Ranking (BPR). While BPR works well for the recommendation task, we find that it causes attribute embeddings to collapse to near-random geometry -- a silent failure that leaves standard ranking metrics largely unaffected and therefore invisible to conventional evaluation. This in turn pollutes user node embeddings, which are shaped by both edge types simultaneously, hurting downstream tasks like personalization, segmentation, etc. Here we propose a Cardinality-Decomposed Loss (CDL) that combines both Cross Entropy (CE) and BPR to enable the model to collectively optimize for relations across cardinalities. We confirm this CE-BPR conflict by showing the two losses compete in the shared encoder's parameter space. We evaluate CDL on five datasets spanning two structural configurations -- one-to-one attributes on user nodes (MovieLens-1M, Last.fm-360K, PayPal Audience Factory, BookCrossing) and on item nodes (Yelp) -- and find that CDL consistently improves discriminability in attribute embeddings. We also show that ranking (NDCG) improves when attributes carry meaningful preference signal, but conflicts with it when the correlation is weak. We use a lambda parameter to navigate this trade-off, and a lambda-sweep reveals that dataset behavior is governed by two graph properties -- semantic alignment and topology leakage. Semantic alignment measures whether the attribute predicts preferences, while topology leakage measures whether the graph's connectivity already encodes it.

</details>


### [298] [Adaptive Confidence-weighted Expansion for Trustworthy Multi-Omics Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20742)
*Mohammad Raahemi,Ali Sekhavati,Alireza Maleki,Hamid Nasiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE框架，通过生成互补模态和双重置信度机制（自适应重加权+全局信任评分），有效提升多模态融合模型在噪声数据下的鲁棒性和可信度，在四个多组学数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法缺乏动态评估数据质量和提供可信置信度分数的机制，导致模型在噪声或非信息性数据流下性能下降，阻碍其在安全关键场景（如医学预后）中的部署。

Method: ACE框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 通过模态内相关性生成新的互补模态，增强多模态空间；(2) 双重置信度机制，包括在融合前根据可靠性自适应重新加权所有模态，以及在融合后对最终决策估计全局信任分数。

Result: 在BRCA、KIPAN、LGG和ROSMAP四个多组学数据集上，ACE在分类性能和置信度校准两方面均显著优于现有最先进算法。

Conclusion: ACE提供了一种更稳定鲁棒的数据融合方法，有助于推动多模态学习在高风险问题中的应用。

Abstract: Multimodal learning is a robust approach to improve predictive performance in applications such as medical prognosis. However, the clinical applicability of models that use multimodal learning is hampered by their poor performance under noisy or uninformative data streams. Present fusion approaches often lack robust mechanisms for the dynamic assessment of data quality and for the provision of a trustable confidence score on the final prediction. This dissuades their deployment in safety-critical settings. To address these limitations, we introduce Adaptive Confidence-weighted Expansion (ACE), a novel framework to enhance the trustworthiness of multimodal fusion models. ACE first enhances the multimodal space by generating new, complementary modalities from intra-modality correlations. It then employs a dual-level confidence mechanism that (1) adaptively reweighs all modalities by their reliability before fusion and (2) estimates a global trust score over the fused, final decision. To evaluate ACE, we used four challenging multi-omics datasets (BRCA, KIPAN, LGG, and ROSMAP). ACE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art algorithms in both classification performance and confidence calibration. Our framework provides a more stable and robust data fusion method that facilitates the use of multimodal learning in addressing high-stakes problems.

</details>


### [299] [GaugeQuant: Online Learning of Quantization-Optimal Bases from LLM Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20757)
*Miguel P. Bento,João Seabra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GaugeQuant方法，通过在训练中引入LogSumExp项打破Transformer内部连续对称性，选择最小化激活异常值的基，实现W4A4量化下LLaMA-2 7B困惑度从8.22降至6.73，无需校准数据和量化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer内部存在不影响输出但改变量化的连续对称性，导致激活异常值问题影响低精度量化性能。现有后训练量化方法需要冻结模型和校准数据集，缺乏在训练中主动优化量化基的方法。

Method: 在训练损失中添加LogSumExp项来打破内部连续对称性；使用stop-gradient算子确保只更新旋转矩阵，不改变语言建模目标；无需特定校准数据、无需量化模拟，仅增加极小训练开销。

Result: LLaMA-2 7B模型上，W4A4（group size 128）困惑度从8.22降至6.73；W4A16困惑度从11.16降至5.45，与需要校准数据集的后训练量化方法效果相当。

Conclusion: GaugeQuant是一种轻量级、无需校准数据的训练中量化方法，通过打破对称性选择最优基，显著降低低精度量化下的困惑度，且不改变语言建模目标。

Abstract: Transformers are known to have internal continuous symmetries that leave outputs invariant, while modifying quantization. GaugeQuant leverages this in-training by introducing a LogSumExp term to the loss that breaks the symmetries, thus selecting a basis that minimizes activation outliers. A stop-gradient operator ensures that only rotation matrices are updated, yielding the language modeling objective completely unaltered. Our requires no specific calibration data, no quantization simulation, and adds negligible training overhead. With the LLaMA-2 7B model under W4A4 quantization with group size 128, perplexity drops from 8.22 to 6.73, competing with post-training methods that require frozen models and calibration datasets. Under W4A16, perplexity drops from 11.16 to 5.45. Code is available at https://github.com/MPedraBento/gauge-quant.

</details>


### [300] [Memory-Computation Tradeoffs in Semi Amortized Parametric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20769)
*Shijie Pan,Agustin Castellano,Zeyu Shen,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线决策系统中，如何用有限的离线存储（记忆）和固定的在线迭代步数K，达到期望精度ε。针对光滑凸参数优化问题，给出了强凸和β-增长条件下记忆与精度、计算量之间的匹配上下界，并揭示了加速所需的记忆成本与优化器收敛率、解映射Lipschitz敏感度的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于离线数据/计算来降低在线计算成本的决策系统取得了经验成功，但关于在固定在线计算预算下，需要多少离线信息才能达到期望精度，缺乏通用的理论理解。

Method: 采用分期参数优化框架：离线阶段存储已求解实例的有限记忆库，在线阶段检索暖启动后执行K步投影梯度下降。分析工具包括非参数预测器、匹配上下界证明框架，并识别出两个关键量：在线优化器收敛率与解映射对参数的Lipschitz敏感度。

Result: 对于μ-强凸目标，给出了记忆量的匹配上下界；对于β-增长条件（β>2）的凸目标，得到近似匹配的界，并发现K超过某阈值后增加记忆不再有益。实验验证了记忆-计算-精度的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 量化了加速所需的记忆成本，建立了离线记忆、在线计算与精度之间的理论权衡，为学习型决策系统的资源分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Learning-enabled decision systems often use offline data or computation to reduce online compute cost. Despite the empirical success of such approaches, there is limited general understanding of how much offline information is needed to achieve a desired accuracy under a fixed online computation budget. We study this question through the lens of amortized parametric optimization: an offline phase stores a finite memory of solved problem instances, and an online phase produces a solution to a new instance by retrieving a warm start and applying $K$ steps of projected gradient descent. We analyze this setup for smooth convex parametric optimization over a compact domain, using a nonparametric predictor built from the stored offline solutions. For $μ$-strongly convex objectives, we establish matching upper and lower bounds on the memory required to guarantee $\varepsilon$-accuracy under a fixed online iteration budget $K$. For convex objectives satisfying a $β$-growth condition ($β>2$), we obtain near-matching bounds and identify a phase transition in $K$ beyond which additional memory provides no benefit. We further provide a general proof framework that (i) explicitly quantifies the memory cost of acceleration---how much offline memory is required to achieve a prescribed speedup over the unaided online optimizer---and (ii) identifies two key quantities driving this cost: the convergence rate of the online optimizer and the Lipschitz sensitivity of the solution map to the problem parameter. Experiments on parameterized ridge regression confirm the predicted memory--computation--accuracy tradeoffs.

</details>


### [301] [Toward Mechanistic Interpretability of an AI Foundation Model Fine-Tuned for Atmospheric Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20778)
*Jason Y. Hu,Ivan Higuera-Mendieta,Patrick Obin Sturm,Makoto M. Kelp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了微软Aurora模型（经微调用于大气化学预测的基础模型）学到了什么。通过控制化学扰动的测试和内部表征分析，发现该模型虽然能捕捉一阶臭氧响应，但未能强制执行基于过程的化学约束，内部表征仍主要围绕预训练期间继承的气象学组织，缺乏化学特有结构。研究提出应通过内部机制而非仅凭基准技能来评判AI成分预测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报基础模型越来越多地被微调用于空气质量预测，成本低于传统化学传输模型，但高预测技能并不揭示模型是否学习了物理机制还是利用了训练数据的统计规律。因此需要研究这类模型到底学到了什么。

Method: 对微软Aurora模型施加受控化学扰动，测试其是否符合已知光化学关系；检查生成这些预测的内部表征；使用稀疏自编码器识别因果控制化学预测的内部组件。

Result: Aurora能捕捉一阶臭氧对活性氮的响应，但不强制执行基于过程模型的化学约束；产生化学不一致的组合；将局部排放特征（如野火羽流）向背景松弛；内部表征主要围绕预训练继承的气象学组织，缺乏化学特有结构；稀疏自编码器识别的内部组件因果控制化学预测但未能清晰映射到单个大气过程。

Conclusion: AI成分预测系统不应仅凭基准技能评判，还应评判其内部机制。该工作为测试AI预测系统是否从再分析数据中学习大气化学提供了框架。

Abstract: Weather forecasting foundation models (FMs) are increasingly fine-tuned to predict air quality, offering fast global pollution forecasts at lower computational cost than conventional chemical transport models. These FMs are typically trained on reanalysis data and generate forecasts through autoregressive rollout. They do not explicitly represent governing physical or chemical processes. Therefore, high forecast skill does not reveal whether a model has learned physical mechanisms or exploits statistical regularities in its training data. Here, we present the first study of what a FM fine-tuned for atmospheric chemistry has learned by examining Microsoft's Aurora model. We impose controlled chemical perturbations on its forecasts and test them against known photochemical relationships. We then examine the internal representations that generate these forecasts. We find that Aurora captures a first-order ozone response to reactive nitrogen but does not enforce the chemical constraints that a process-based model encodes. It generates chemically inconsistent combinations of related species and relaxes localized emission features such as wildfire plumes toward background. Internally, its representations remain largely organized around the meteorology inherited during pretraining, with little structure specific to chemistry. Using sparse autoencoders, we identify internal components that causally control the chemical forecast but do not map cleanly onto individual atmospheric processes. This work provides a framework for testing whether AI forecasting systems learn atmospheric chemistry from reanalysis data. As these models are increasingly positioned to inform environmental policy decisions, we argue that composition forecasts should also be judged by their internal mechanisms rather than by benchmark skill alone.

</details>


### [302] [Synthetic minority data is redundant or invalid: a data-dependent validity theory and a de-biased test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20787)
*Ahmad B. Hassanat,Ahmad S. Tarawneh,Ghada A. Altarawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出合成少数类样本的标准验证方法存在偏差（用生成数据自评），提出了去偏评估框架：用留出真实数据估计合成样本的群体有效性。证明有效性由数据重叠程度决定而非生成方法，实验表明91种方法均无法同时满足有效性和信息增益，中位F1提升不足0.01且损害校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类不平衡学习中合成少数类样本的验证方法存在系统性偏差——使用生成数据本身进行验证会导致无效样本被误判为有效，需要建立无偏的评估框架。

Method: 提出去偏验证方法：将有效性定义为群体概率（合成点真实属于少数类的概率），用留出的真实数据作为一致估计量；理论上证明类重叠程度决定了有效性的下限。

Result: 经典测试在96-99%的方法-不平衡比组合中低估了真实无效性；91种方法在3个分类器、医学和金融数据集上均未同时满足有效性和信息增益两个标准，中位F1提升低于0.01且多数损害校准。

Conclusion: 合成少数类数据的有效性是数据的属性而非方法的属性；类重叠设定了不可逾越的无效性下限；举证责任应转移：合成数据必须证明有效性和信息增益。

Abstract: For two decades, the standard remedy for class-imbalanced learning has been to fabricate synthetic minority examples, and the standard evidence of their validity has been a check that cannot fail: synthetic points are scored against the very data that generated them. We de-bias the check. Validity becomes a population quantity -- the probability that a synthetic point truly belongs to the minority class -- with a consistent estimator that scores synthetic points against withheld real data. Where held-out ground truth is available, the classical test underestimates true invalidity in 96-99% of method-by-imbalance-ratio cells, while the de-biased estimator tracks it closely. We prove validity is a property of the data, not the method: class overlap sets an invalidity floor no faithful generator escapes, making oversampling redundant where classes separate and invalid where they overlap. Across 91 methods, three classifiers, and datasets spanning medicine and finance -- including a generator engineered to pass the classical check -- none clears both bars: gains over the best trivial baseline are noise-thin (median below 0.01 F1, a decision threshold's reach), and most damage calibration. We release the audit as a pip-installable test and flip the burden of proof: synthetic minority data must now demonstrate, on the data at hand, both validity and information gain.

</details>


### [303] [Memoir: Should a Model Write to Its Memory While It Thinks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20792)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memoir是一种结合快速内存、慢速共享参数、可变深度潜在递归和未来潜在能量目标的记忆增强方法。实验发现，在程序化联想召回任务中，读写耦合版本（允许每次迭代重写同一次迭代读取的快速层）相比只读版本存在学习速度惩罚（240步后召回率0.5203 vs 0.6557），但在960步后两者均达到1.0，表明该惩罚仅限于固定预算下的学习速度，而非能力限制。内存重写未如预期破坏能量信号，能量裕度持续增长。此外，内核重构将delta-rule前向时间从0.907ms降至0.351ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试Memoir中最具风险的耦合设计：每次池化迭代重写该迭代读取的快速层，探究这种读写耦合是否会导致性能下降或能量信号破坏。

Method: 设计配对实验，比较读写耦合版与只读版在程序化联想召回任务上的表现。两臂参数量相同（81,738参数，76,362可训练），使用匹配的前向乘加计数、数据、优化器、调度和随机种子。240步时12个种子，960步时8个种子。

Result: 240步后，耦合版召回率0.5203（95% CI [0.4522, 0.5883]），只读版0.6557（[0.5953, 0.7160]），差异0.1354（配对t=3.23，df=11，95% CI [0.0431, 0.2277]），10/12种子只读胜出。960步后两臂均达1.0。能量裕度持续增长，未出现预期中的信号破坏。内核重构将delta-rule前向时间从0.907ms降至0.351ms。

Conclusion: 读写耦合带来学习速度惩罚但非能力限制，能量信号未如预期被破坏。控制组因天花板效应受限，需要非饱和任务进一步验证。内核重构显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Memoir combines per-sample fast memory, shared slow parameters, variable-depth latent recurrence, and a future-latent energy objective. We test its riskiest coupling: each pondering iteration may rewrite the fast tier that the same iteration reads. On procedural associative recall with key interference, we compare a coupled arm against an otherwise identical read-only pondering arm. Both arms contain 81,738 parameters, including 76,362 trainable parameters, and use matched declared forward multiply-accumulate counts, data, optimizer, schedule, and seeds. After 240 training steps across 12 seeds, coupled recall is 0.5203 with a 95 percent interval of [0.4522, 0.5883], while read-only recall is 0.6557 with [0.5953, 0.7160]. The arms are paired per seed, and the read-only lead of 0.1354 gives a paired t of 3.23 on 11 degrees of freedom with a 95 percent interval of [0.0431, 0.2277] on the difference, winning on 10 of 12 seeds. After 960 steps across 8 seeds, both arms reach 1.0000, so the measured effect is a learning-speed penalty at a fixed budget, not a demonstrated capability penalty. That longer control is ceiling limited, leaving convergence on a non-saturating task unmeasured. A predicted failure in which memory rewriting corrupts the energy signal did not occur: the energy margin grew and held. Kernel restructuring also reduced delta-rule forward time from 0.907 ms to 0.351 ms on the stated device. Code and evidence are available at https://github.com/RightNow-AI/Memoir

</details>


### [304] [Counterfactual Explainability Framework With CycleGAN And Counterfactual-Classifier Alignnment Score for Retinal Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21068)
*Kritanu Chattopadhyay,Sayanjit Singha Roy,Soumya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CounterFundus是一个基于CycleGAN的反事实可解释性框架，通过将病理性眼底图像转换为估计的健康对照图像并生成差异图来定位疾病相关视网膜变化，同时提出Counterfactual-Classifier Alignment Score (CCAS)量化空间一致性，实验证明该框架在眼底疾病检测中既提供可解释性又提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在眼底疾病检测中表现优异，但其缺乏可解释性，特别是模型决策未与临床有意义的视网膜区域关联时，成为临床采用的主要瓶颈。现有后验显著性方法（如saliency maps）难以提供直观且临床可信的解释。

Method: 1) 采用EfficientNet-B5作为视网膜疾病检测分类器；2) 利用CycleGAN生成器将病理性眼底图像转换为估计的健康对照图像（反事实解释）；3) 通过原始图像与生成健康图像的差异图定位疾病相关视网膜变化；4) 提出CCAS指标（融合Spearman相关、二元IoU和指向精度）量化反事实差异图与分类器显著图的空间一致性；5) 使用EigenCAM进行对齐评估；6) 进行消融实验验证CCAS过滤的反事实增强对下游分类性能的提升。

Result: EigenCAM对齐评估表明，生成的反事实解释在所有CCAS维度上与分类器相关的视网膜证据保持空间一致性。消融研究证实，CCAS过滤的反事实增强可改善下游眼底图像分类性能。

Conclusion: CounterFundus作为临床可信的可解释人工智能框架，通过视觉上合理的疾病到正常视网膜转换提供反事实解释，解决了DL模型在眼底疾病检测中的可解释性瓶颈，为临床采纳铺平道路。

Abstract: Automated detection of vision impairing retina-based ocular conditions from fundus images is important for early screening, timely referral and reducing dependency on specialist-only assessment, for which neural network-based deep learning (DL) models have been widely utilized. However, explainability of the DL frameworks remains a major bottleneck for clinical adoption, particularly when model decisions are not linked to retinal regions that are clinically meaningful. To address this issue, this study presents CounterFundus, a novel CycleGAN-driven counterfactual explainability framework, integrating EfficientNet-B5-based retinal disease detection with visually interpretable disease-to-normal fundus image translation. For each pathological image, the counterfactual yielded by the CycleGAN generator represents an estimated healthy counterpart and the resultant difference map is utilized to localize disease-associated retinal changes. Unlike conventional post-hoc saliency methods, CounterFundus provides counterfactual explanations through visually plausible disease-to-normal retinal translation. Thereafter, to quantify the spatial agreement between counterfactual difference maps and classifier saliency, the Counterfactual-Classifier Alignment Score (CCAS) is introduced, embedding Spearman correlation, binary IoU and pointing accuracy into a single assessment protocol. To this end, EigenCAM-aligned evaluation demonstrates that the generated counterfactual explanations remain spatially consistent with classifier-relevant retinal evidence across all CCAS dimensions. Along with that, ablation studies further confirm that CCAS-filtered counterfactual augmentation improves the downstream classification performance in fundus images, establishing CounterFundus as a clinically-grounded, explainable artificially intelligence (XAI) framework for retinal disease detection.

</details>


### [305] [External Clustering Validation by the Homogeneity-Parsimony Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20799)
*Andreas Tiffeau-Mayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息瓶颈原理的聚类同质性与简约性归一化评分，通过数学证明其随聚类细化单调变化，并扩展到集匹配和基于对的版本，统一了常用评估标准，展示了在特征选择和算法比较中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类评估中常用的标量指标会掩盖"聚类应提供类别信息但避免不必要碎片化"这一基本权衡，现有方法缺乏对这一权衡的系统量化。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈原理并修改为不奖励有损压缩，定义聚类同质性和简约性的归一化分数；通过数学证明其单调性；将信息论框架扩展到香农熵之外，推导集匹配和基于对的对应版本。

Result: 新定义的评分随聚类细化单调变化；基于对的设置中，同质性-简约性权衡恢复为二元分类器的ROC曲线；统一了多种常用评估标准。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于特征选择和算法比较，通过联合考虑评分可明确聚类操作点并识别帕累托最优解。

Abstract: Scalar metrics are often used to evaluate clusterings against known classes, but they can obscure a fundamental trade-off: clusterings should be informative about class labels while avoiding unnecessary fragmentation. Here we describe normalized scores of cluster homogeneity and parsimony that quantify this trade-off. These scores build on the information bottleneck principle, modified to not reward lossy compression. We show by example and mathematical proof that our definitions of these scores have the intuitive property of varying monotonically under cluster refinement in contrast to related proposals. Extending the information-theoretic framework beyond Shannon entropies, we furthermore derive set-matching and pair-based counterparts of the homogeneity and parsimony scores. These unify commonly used evaluation criteria and show that, in the pair-based setting, the homogeneity-parsimony trade-off recovers the receiver operating characteristic of binary classifiers. We demonstrate the framework's utility for feature selection and algorithm comparison, illustrating how considering scores jointly can clarify clustering operating points and identify Pareto-optimal solutions.

</details>


### [306] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M³-Gen框架，通过条件生成对抗网络结合组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，利用对比学习统一多模态表征，并在TCGA数据集上验证了其生成真实、功能相关基因表达的能力，同时通过注意力机制提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据的获取受限于高昂成本和隐私问题，阻碍了多模态研究和AI驱动应用的发展；现有方法难以有效整合临床元数据、组织病理学图像和分子谱等多源异质生物医学数据。

Method: 提出M³-Gen框架，采用条件生成对抗网络（GAN），以组织病理学图像和临床元数据为条件生成基因表达谱；通过对比学习从临床变量和图像中学习统一潜在表征；利用注意力机制融合多模态嵌入，引导生成模型产生生物学上一致的基因表达数据。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上的评估表明，M³-Gen能够生成真实且功能上有意义的基因表达数据；通过注意力机制，模型能够识别组织病理学图像中哪些区域对特定基因表达谱的生成影响最大，提供了内生的可解释性。

Conclusion: M³-Gen框架成功实现了利用组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成生物学合理的基因表达谱，解决了多模态数据整合中的基因表达缺失问题，且其基于注意力的设计使模型决策具有可解释性，为计算病理学和精准医学提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [307] [New Complexity-Theoretic Frontiers of Tractability for Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20811)
*Cornelius Brand,Robert Ganian,Mathis Rocton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性激活和ReLU激活神经网络最优训练的计算复杂度，提出了新的多项式时间可解架构。对于ReLU网络，证明了所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构是多项式时间可解的；对于线性激活网络，首次识别了满足数据吞吐量条件的非平凡多项式时间可解网络类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络在机器学习中至关重要，但即使是最简单的激活函数，其最优训练的计算复杂度仍不完全清楚。现有研究主要集中于建立下界，而在识别新的多项式时间可解网络架构方面进展有限。

Method: 针对ReLU网络，扩展了Arora等人的算法，证明所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构可在多项式时间内最优训练；针对线性激活网络，提出了一种利用数据吞吐量条件的新算法，首次识别出非平凡的多项式时间可解网络类。

Result: 对于ReLU网络，将多项式时间可解性从先前已知的架构推广到所有隐藏神经元出度为1的架构。对于线性激活网络，首次发现满足数据吞吐量条件的网络架构可在多项式时间内最优训练。

Conclusion: 本文建立了新的算法上界，推动了线性激活和ReLU激活神经网络最优训练的可解性边界，超越了现有技术水平。

Abstract: In spite of the fundamental role of neural networks in contemporary machine learning research, our understanding of the computational complexity of optimally training neural networks remains incomplete even when dealing with the simplest kinds of activation functions. Indeed, while there has been a number of very recent results that establish ever-tighter lower bounds for the problem under linear and ReLU activation functions, less progress has been made towards the identification of novel polynomial-time tractable network architectures. In this article we obtain novel algorithmic upper bounds for training linear- and ReLU-activated neural networks to optimality which push the boundaries of tractability for these problems beyond the previous state of the art. In particular, for ReLU networks we establish the polynomial-time tractability of all architectures where hidden neurons have an out-degree of $1$, improving upon the previous algorithm of Arora, Basu, Mianjy and Mukherjee. On the other hand, for networks with linear activation functions we identify the first non-trivial polynomial-time solvable class of networks by obtaining an algorithm that can optimally train network architectures satisfying a novel data throughput condition.

</details>


### [308] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构可逆变换的扩散Transformer后训练量化方法，在W4A4量化下相比现有方法（SmoothQuant、SVDQuant、LoRaQ）实现了更好的量化质量和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在W4A4后训练量化时，激活值中的异常值导致4-bit格式无法准确表示，现有方法（如SmoothQuant、Hadamard变换、全维可逆变换）在量化精度和推理计算成本之间存在权衡，缺乏既能保持高量化精度又具有低在线计算开销的方案。

Method: KroQuant对激活值的每个32元素块应用学习得到的Kronecker结构可逆变换（相比per-channel scaling存储参数更少），利用小张量核心GEMM实现块局部运算；同时通过离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快14%；在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell的W4A4（MXFP4e2）设置下，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI上生成的结果比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换在量化精度和推理效率之间取得了更好的平衡，是扩散Transformer后训练量化的有效方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [309] [Robust Asynchronous Q-Learning under Reward and State Corruption via Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20822)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BR-Async-Q算法，一种基于epoch的鲁棒Q学习算法，通过数据分批和鲁棒贝尔曼最优算子估计来抵御奖励和状态观测的对抗性污染，首次为异步Q学习在双重 corruption 下提供了鲁棒性保证，且在仅奖励被污染时达到极小极大最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在恶劣环境中面临对抗性数据污染问题，现有方法缺乏对异步Q学习在同时存在奖励和状态污染下的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 提出BR-Async-Q算法：(i) 将在线数据流分批处理以降低方差，(ii) 基于批数据构建鲁棒的贝尔曼最优算子估计。

Result: 证明了BR-Async-Q的高概率ℓ∞误差界，与标准Q学习匹配，仅多出一个与被污染样本比例相关的附加项。在仅奖励被污染时，算法误差界对污染比例的依赖达到极小极大最优。

Conclusion: BR-Async-Q首次为同时存在奖励和状态污染的异步Q学习提供了鲁棒性保证，且在仅奖励污染场景下达到最优。

Abstract: Motivated by reinforcement learning in harsh environments, we consider the problem of learning an optimal policy subject to adversarially corrupted feedback. Specifically, at each time-step, an adversary can perturb both the reward and state observations of the learner following the Huber contamination model. To defend against such data corruption, we propose {\texttt{BR-Async-Q}}: a novel, epoch-based, robust \(Q\)-learning algorithm built upon two key ideas: (i) partitioning the online data stream into batches to reduce variance, and (ii) constructing robust estimates of the Bellman optimality operator using such batched data. We prove a high-probability $\ell_\infty$ error bound for {\texttt{BR-Async-Q}} that matches that for vanilla \(Q\)-learning, up to a small additive term that scales with the fraction of corrupted samples. To our knowledge, this provides the first robustness guarantee for asynchronous \(Q\)-learning subject to both reward and state corruption. Furthermore, when only rewards are corrupted, the dependence of our algorithm's bound on the corruption fraction is minimax optimal.

</details>


### [310] [Offline RL with Hierarchical Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20834)
*Ahad Jawaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiQC提出了一种结合高层潜在规划与低层动作分块的离线目标条件强化学习算法，通过低层评论家条件化为时间扩展动作序列，实现了无偏的k步值备份，从理论和实验上证明其相比传统层级或平面分块方法在长时域任务上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习在长时域任务中面临时域诅咒，即值估计误差通过长链Bellman备份累积；现有层级方法虽分解任务为子目标，但底层控制器存在短视执行和有偏值估计的问题。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Implicit Q-Chunking (HiQC)，包含高层潜在规划与低层动作分块；低层评论家条件化为时间扩展动作序列，实现无偏k步值备份；在规划和执行两个层面同时压缩时域。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法在每步备份有界误差模型下比标准层级或平面分块更紧的值误差界；实验上在OGBench测试套件中取得了最高的综合性能，在长时域导航任务（如humanoid-giant）上增益最大。

Conclusion: HiQC通过双重分解有效缓解了离线目标条件强化学习中的时域诅咒问题，在长时域任务上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) holds the promise of learning general-purpose policies from static datasets. However, scaling these methods to long-horizon tasks remains a challenge due to the curse of horizon, where value estimation errors can compound through long chains of bootstrapped Bellman backups. Existing hierarchical approaches mitigate this by decomposing tasks into subgoals, yet they often rely on low-level controllers that suffer from myopic execution and biased value estimates. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Implicit Q-Chunking (HiQC), an offline goal-conditioned RL algorithm that combines high-level latent planning with low-level action chunking. By conditioning the low-level critic on temporally extended action sequences, HiQC enables unbiased k-step value backups, compressing the horizon at both the planning and execution levels. We theoretically demonstrate that this dual decomposition results in a tighter bound on value error under a bounded per-backup error model compared to standard hierarchy or flat chunking alone. Empirically, HiQC achieves the highest aggregate performance among the compared methods on the OGBench suite, with its largest gains on long-horizon navigation tasks such as humanoid-giant.

</details>


### [311] [Multilevel Graph Wavelet Compressed Sensing with Scale-Aware Neural Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20857)
*Amirhossein Nouranizadeh,Sarang Rajendra Patil,Alan John Varghese,Varsha Narayanan,Amit Chakraborty,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图小波压缩感知（GWCS）框架，通过谱图小波变换将图信号表示为稀疏、可解释的小波域表示，结合非参数多级重要性采样器和尺度感知图神经网络，实现对网格和随机图上的PDE仿真数据的高保真压缩，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习（如神经算子、物理信息神经网络）在工程应用和逆问题中取得了进展，但其训练通常需要大量模拟数据，导致数据准备和模型训练成本高昂。因此，需要一种高效的离线数据压缩方法来降低存储和传输开销。

Method: 提出Graph Wavelet Compressed Sensing (GWCS)框架：(1) 使用谱图小波变换将图信号变换到小波域；(2) 设计非参数多级重要性采样器，在给定压缩比下保留每个尺度内的高能量小波系数；(3) 使用尺度感知图神经网络从稀疏系数重建原始信号。

Result: 在合成近似带限图信号和四个PDE仿真数据集（Turbulent Radiative Layer, Viscoelastic Instability, Kolmogorov Flow, Dynamic Stall）上，与图信号采样方法和图自编码器基线相比，GWCS实现了更高的重建保真度和显著的数据压缩。

Conclusion: GWCS框架能够高效压缩图信号，在保持高重建质量的同时实现大幅数据压缩，为科学机器学习中的数据瓶颈问题提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning methods such as neural operators and physics-informed neural networks have advanced engineering applications and inverse problems, but their training typically requires large volumes of simulated data. This makes data preparation and model training expensive. We propose Graph Wavelet Compressed Sensing (GWCS), a learning-based framework for offline compression of graph signals by representing them as sparse, interpretable wavelet-domain representations using the spectral graph wavelet transform. The framework combines a nonparametric multilevel importance sampler, which retains high-energy wavelet coefficients within each scale for a given compression ratio, with a scale-aware graph neural network that reconstructs the signal from the sparse coefficients. We evaluate the proposed framework on synthetic approximately band-limited graph signals over random graphs and four PDE simulation datasets over meshes, which include Turbulent Radiative Layer, Viscoelastic Instability, Kolmogorov Flow, and Dynamic Stall. We compare against graph signal sampling methods and graph autoencoder baselines. Results demonstrate that the framework achieves high reconstruction fidelity and substantial data compression compared to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [312] [Position Bias is Hidden Behind Ceiling Effects: A Permutation Diagnostic for LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20864)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍inspect_permute工具，通过对24,000次API调用进行穷举答案顺序排列测试，发现LLM多选题位置偏差仅在60-95%准确率的Goldilocks区间内可检测，低于该区间受处理负载主导、高于该区间受天花板效应影响；标准MMLU基准因位于检测带之外，无法测量前沿模型的真实位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多选题评估中位置偏差的测量方法仅使用单次答案顺序打乱，无法区分偏差信号与内容噪声和采样随机性，亟需一种系统化的统计检验方法来准确量化位置偏差的显著性。

Method: 开发inspect_permute开源工具，对每个问题执行穷举答案顺序排列，使用卡方检验/Cramér V统计量结合bootstrap置信区间检测位置偏差；在四个厂商模型（gpt-4o-mini, claude-haiku-4-5, gemini-2.5-flash, grok-3）的五个MMLU子集上进行24,000次API调用（温度=0），并以SHA-256哈希预注册伪造者预测。

Result: 位置偏差仅在60-95%准确率的Goldilocks区间内可检测；低于该区间受处理负载主导（processing-load dominance），高于该区间天花板效应使方差压缩至卡方检验分辨率以下；可检测的偏差分为两种机制：单调A→D递减（处理负载型，低层级模型）和非单调D-drop（内容模糊型，狭窄能力带）。标准MMLU将前沿模型置于检测带之上，因此无偏差信号应解读为不可测量而非无偏差。

Conclusion: 本研究与arXiv:2606.26185共同界定了位置偏差测量的可检测区域，使该领域核心问题可以以可验证的形式提出。工具、数据和预注册信息均以MIT协议开源发布。

Abstract: Position bias in multiple-choice LLM evaluation is widely cited as a confound in capability comparisons, but published measurements rely on single answer-order shuffles whose results confound the bias signal with content-level noise and sampling stochasticity. I introduce inspect_permute, an open-source extension to the inspect_ai evaluation framework that runs exhaustive answer-order permutations per question and reports the chi-squared / Cramer V signature of position bias with bootstrap confidence intervals. I apply the tool across four vendors (gpt-4o-mini, claude-haiku-4-5, gemini-2.5-flash, grok-3) on five MMLU subjects, 24,000 API calls under temperature-0 generation, with falsifier predictions pre-registered via a public SHA-256 hash before half the data was observed. Position bias turns out to be statistically detectable only within a roughly 60-95% base-accuracy Goldilocks zone. Below it, processing-load dominance swamps subject-specific signal; above it, ceiling effects compress the variance below the chi-squared test resolution. Detectable cells separate into two mechanism types: monotone A-to-D decrease (processing_load, in low-tier models) and non-monotone D-drop (content_ambiguity, in a narrow capability band). Standard MMLU places every frontier-tier model above the detection band, so absence of signal there should be read as not measurable, not unbiased. Together with the ceiling-effect characterisation in arXiv:2606.26185, this work brackets the detectable region of position-bias measurement and makes the field central question askable in a verifiable form. Package, data, preregistration under MIT.

</details>


### [313] [TwistedMerge: Certified Higher-Order Diagnostics and Abstention for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20887)
*Ting Gong,Shitan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TwistedMerge，一个基于下降理论的模型合并认证框架，通过将合并问题形式化为有限下降问题，利用同调论工具检测合并中的全局不一致性，并在无法认证时选择弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型合并方法依赖成对对齐，但成对可对齐性不能保证全局一致性对齐，导致合并后的模型可能存在隐式冲突。现有方法缺乏对合并结果可靠性的形式化验证机制。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为有限下降问题：检查点为局部对象，对齐映射为转移，循环积为残差。提出TwistedMerge认证流程，包括固定图表平均、同步可移除的规范不一致、指定比较复形上的认证中心障碍和非阿贝尔和乐。残差需通过逆一致性、系数识别、中心性和闭包测试才能提升为上同调类，否则弃权并回退到普通或同步融合。

Result: 证明了常数边不可行结果、冻结复形三路和前声明族误差控制定理，以及比较复形敏感性的细化测试。人工植入的神经对齐缺陷可通过循环一致同步移除，表明非零循环分数本身不是更高阶障碍。受控中心系统恢复了预测的非上边界和射影秩行为。在自然检查点集合上，循环残差不能预测合并退化，且未认证出自然中心类或周期指标类。

Conclusion: 下降理论可作为可证伪的认证和弃权框架，为模型合并提供了严格的理论保障和实用的安全机制，在无法保证一致性时通过弃权避免不可靠合并。

Abstract: Model merging combines independently trained or fine-tuned models, but pairwise alignability does not imply globally consistent alignment. We formulate merging as a finite descent problem in which checkpoints are local objects, alignment maps are transitions, and cycle products are residuals. TwistedMerge is a conservative certification pipeline that separates fixed-chart averaging, synchronization-removable gauge inconsistency, a certified central obstruction on a specified comparison complex, and nonabelian holonomy. A residual is promoted to a cohomology class only after inverse-consistency, coefficient-identification, centrality, and closure tests; otherwise the method abstains and returns an ordinary or synchronized fallback. We prove a constant-edge no-go result, frozen-complex three-way and predeclared-family error-control theorems, and a refinement test for comparison-complex sensitivity. A planted neural alignment defect is removed by cycle-consistent synchronization, showing that a nonzero cycle score alone is not a higher obstruction. Controlled central systems recover the predicted non-coboundary and projective-rank behavior, while noisy estimates move from certification to abstention without false lifts on the tested controls. A trained low-rank-adapter audit shows that naive factor averaging depends on the chosen GLr representative, whereas global factor synchronization and dense-delta SVD are stable. On natural checkpoint collections, cycle residuals do not predict merge degradation and no natural central or period-index class is certified. The results position descent theory as a falsifiable certification and abstention framework.

</details>


### [314] [Information-Theoretically Secure Aggregation for Lightweight Federated Learning: Resilient to Dropouts and Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20890)
*Hyeong-Gun Joo,Songnam Hong,Dong-Joon Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向符号型联邦学习的轻量级信息论安全聚合框架，通过逆形式指数约减和单轮安全乘法两项关键技术，在保证信息论安全性的同时，将在线通信量降低99.5%、延迟降低85.7%，并利用MDS码解码实现了对退出和对抗行为的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号型联邦学习（如signSGD）传输单比特梯度以降低通信开销，但其梯度符号易受推理攻击，而现有安全聚合方案与符号方法不兼容或开销过大。因此，需要一种轻量级且信息论安全的聚合框架。

Method: 提出一个信息论安全聚合框架：1）通过单轮安全乘法安全计算多数投票多项式；2）逆形式指数约减（inverse-form exponent reduction）将有效多项式度数减半；3）单轮安全乘法实现线性离线复杂度和存储，仅需一轮在线通信；4）基于MDS码解码实现鲁棒性。

Result: 在线通信量降低99.5%，延迟降低85.7%；在退出和对抗行为下分别提升准确率20.65%和10.74%。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模、低延迟、信息论安全的符号型联邦学习聚合建立了实用基础。

Abstract: On-device federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving and personalized model training on resource-constrained devices such as smartphones and IoT nodes. To reduce communication cost, sign-based methods (e.g., signSGD) transmit one-bit gradients. However, exposing gradient signs makes them vulnerable to inference attacks, while existing secure aggregation schemes are often incompatible with such methods or incur significant computational and communication overhead. We propose a lightweight and information-theoretically secure aggregation framework tailored for sign-based FL. The framework securely computes the majority vote (MV) polynomial through single-round secure multiplication, ensuring end-to-end information-theoretic security under the honest-majority assumption while revealing only the final aggregated sign to the server. To enhance efficiency and scalability, we introduce two key techniques. First, inverse-form exponent reduction halves the effective MV polynomial degree, reducing both communication and computation costs. Second, we propose single-round secure multiplication, achieving linear offline complexity and storage with only a single online communication. Together, these techniques reduce online communication by up to 99.5% and latency by up to 85.7% compared to conventional approaches. Also, by leveraging inherent MDS-code-based decoding, the framework achieves robustness against both dropouts and adversarial behaviors, yielding accuracy gains of up to 20.65% and 10.74%, respectively. Overall, the proposed framework establishes a practical foundation for large-scale, low-latency, and information-theoretically secure aggregation in sign-based FL.

</details>


### [315] [HierarchicalDAEW: Domain-Aware Edge-Weighted Graph Convolution with Evidential Uncertainty for Multi-Section Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from H&E Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20896)
*Kritanu Chattopadhyay,Soumya Chatterjee,Ondrej Krejcar,Debotosh Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HierarchicalDAEW是一种双图架构的空间转录组学预测方法，通过域感知边加权卷积和蛋白质-蛋白质交互先验融合，在六个人体组织切片上与13个基线方法相比实现了最佳性能，并提供了校准良好的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学技术成本高昂且技术要求高，限制了其常规临床部署。从H&E染色组织学预测空间分辨基因表达可弥合这一差距，但现有方法大多忽略组织架构且缺乏预测可靠性的量化。

Method: 提出HierarchicalDAEW双图架构：(1) 点图上使用域感知边加权卷积算子(Domain-Aware Edge-Weighted)，分别学习域间、域内和边界边的投影，利用Leiden聚类将组织异质性作为显式结构信号；(2) 基因级图通过注意力门控融合STRING-DB蛋白质-蛋白质交互先验与组织特异性共表达，从标志基因集传播到更广泛的基因面板；(3) 采用证据不确定性估计生成校准良好的置信区间。

Result: 在六个Visium人类切片(乳腺、结直肠、前列腺、小脑)上，与13个已发布基线相比，HierarchicalDAEW实现了与真实表达最强相关性，多种子可重复性检验和阴性对照均验证了性能提升。消融实验确认了域感知边类型和分层深度的必要性。

Conclusion: HierarchicalDAEW通过显式建模组织异质性和提供校准的不确定性估计，实现了更准确的基因表达预测，其低置信度预测可交由病理学家审查后再进行临床决策。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics assays remain costly and technically demanding, restricting transcriptome-wide profiling to specialist settings and preventing routine clinical deployment. Predicting spatially resolved gene expression from H&E histology could close this gap, yet current methods largely ignore the underlying tissue architecture and rarely quantify how their predictions can be trusted. We introduce HierarchicalDAEW, a dual-graph architecture that addresses both gaps. On the spot graph, a Domain-Aware Edge-Weighted convolutional operator learns separate projections for inter-domain, intra-domain, and boundary edges derived from Leiden clustering, allowing the model to treat tissue heterogeneity as an explicit structural signal rather than an implicit one. A second gene-level graph then fuses protein-protein interaction priors from STRING-DB with tissue-specific co-expression through learned attention gating, propagating predictions from a landmark gene set to a broader gene panel. Reliability is handled through evidential uncertainty estimation, which produces far better calibrated confidence intervals than Monte Carlo dropout under identical conditions. Across six human Visium sections spanning breast, colorectal, prostate, and cerebellar tissue, and against thirteen published baselines, HierarchicalDAEW achieves the strongest correlation with ground-truth expression, with gains that hold up under multi-seed reproducibility checks and negative controls that rule out positional shortcuts. Ablations further confirm that both the domain-aware edge typing and the hierarchical depth are necessary to this improvement, and calibrated uncertainty estimates identify low-confidence predictions for pathologist review before clinical action.

</details>


### [316] [Multi-turn RL with Structural and Performance Aware Rewards for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20908)
*Quazi Ishtiaque Mahmud,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CudaPerf提出了一种结合可验证执行奖励和结构感知奖励的反射式强化学习框架，用于生成优化CUDA代码，在C→CUDA和PyTorch→CUDA任务上分别实现了最高5倍和3.32倍的加速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅依赖结果层面的信号（如正确性和加速比），忽略了程序并行化相关的结构属性（如内存合并、占用率、算术强度和同步模式），限制了优化代码的生成能力。

Method: CudaPerf采用两阶段框架：离线阶段通过对比学习训练成对排序模块区分强弱程序候选；在线RL阶段联合优化正确性、性能和结构效率，并利用执行反馈进行迭代精炼。

Result: 在C→CUDA和PyTorch→CUDA任务上，相比Qwen-3-32B和CUDA Agent基线，分别实现最高5倍和3.32倍加速提升，以及17%和7%的正确性改进。

Conclusion: 结合并行化结构特征的结构感知奖励能显著提升LLM生成优化CUDA代码的正确性和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful technique to enhance the reasoning capacity of LLMs for optimized code generation. However, existing RLVR approaches primarily rely on outcome-based signals such as correctness and speedup, overlooking performance-critical structural properties of programs that are essential for generating optimized code. In this work, we propose CudaPerf, a reflective RL framework that incorporates both verifiable execution rewards and structural code-aware rewards derived from parallelization features (e.g., memory coalescing, occupancy, Arithmatic Intensity, and synchronization patterns). CudaPerf operates in two stages: (1) an offline pairwise ranking module that learns to distinguish strong and weak program candidates via contrastive comparisons, and (2) an online RL training phase that jointly optimizes for correctness, performance, and structural efficiency through a unified reward signal. To further enhance learning, CudaPerf utilizes iterative refinement using execution feedback enabling progressive improvement of generated candidates. We also introduce a dataset comprising 2.9k C to CUDA and 1k PyTorch to CUDA programs, each paired with diverse input configurations and multiple CUDA implementations encompassing diverse optimization strategies. CudaPerf is evaluated across multiple benchmarks comprising both C to CUDA and PyTorch to CUDA transformations. Empirical findings suggest that CudaPerf significantly outperforms strong baselines, including Qwen-3-32B (for C to CUDA) and CUDA Agent (for PyTorch to CUDA) by achieving up to 5X & 3.32X improvements in speedup, and 17% & 7% improvements in correctness, respectively.

</details>


### [317] [Three-Pronged Spectral Control for Federated Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20914)
*Shiva Raj Pokhrel,Dipsan Bhattarai,Anwar Walid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRISHUL是一个面向联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）的谱控制框架，通过共享冻结多头低秩基、核范数近端收缩和非均匀层间头分配策略，在非独立同分布客户端异质性下提升收敛稳定性与最终性能，且几乎不增加额外通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦PEFT在非IID客户端异质性下表现脆弱，尤其LoRA中不同客户端学习的更新子空间存在谱错位，导致聚合高方差和全局迁移效果差。

Method: 提出TRISHUL框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 共享冻结多头低秩基实现核心更新的代数精确聚合；2) 核范数近端收缩在上传前抑制客户端特有的高秩谱成分；3) 基于预训练层容量的凹水填充预算规则非均匀分配各层适配头数量。收缩仅在小型核心矩阵上执行，几乎不增加计算和通信开销。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、SVHN、20 Newsgroups、MRQA、GLUE（使用LLaMA3.2-1B）等视觉和语言基准上，TRISHUL在收敛速度、稳定性和最终性能上均优于联邦LoRA基线，异质性越强提升越显著。

Conclusion: TRISHUL通过谱控制有效缓解了联邦PEFT在非IID场景下的异质性退化问题，是一种轻量且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) enables communication-efficient adaptation of large pretrained models on decentralized edge data, but it remains fragile under non-IID client heterogeneity. In low-rank adaptation (LoRA), different clients may learn locally useful but spectrally misaligned update subspaces, causing high-variance aggregation and poor global transfer. We propose TRISHUL, a spectral-control framework for robust federated PEFT. TRISHUL follows the FL no-raw-data-sharing setting but does not itself provide formal privacy guarantees. TRISHUL uses shared frozen multi-head low-rank bases to obtain algebraically exact aggregation of compact core updates, applies nuclear norm proximal shrinkage to suppress client-specific high-rank spectral components before upload, and allocates adaptation heads non-uniformly across layers using a concave water filling budget rule derived from pretrained layer capacity. Because shrinkage is performed only on small core matrices, TRISHUL adds negligible computation and no extra per-round communication over the underlying multi-head PEFT protocol. Across vision and language benchmarks, including CIFAR-100, SVHN, 20 Newsgroups, MRQA, and GLUE with LLaMA3.2-1B, TRISHUL improves convergence, stability, and final performance over federated LoRA baselines, with greater gains under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [318] [Best-of-Evidence: Best-of-N Selection under Partial Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20950)
*Cenwei Zhang,Teng Fang,Yuxia Wang,Derek Li,Bryan Dai,Lei You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Best-of-Evidence (BoE)框架，在部分验证条件下通过签名候选-因子图分配有限证据预算来改进输出选择，理论上证明了证据容量界限和查询分离优势，在医疗VQA任务中展示了有效性及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoN方法假设完整的候选响应可以被可靠评估，但许多视觉-语言任务只提供部分验证（如发现、跨度、数值、区域或关系），缺乏全局可靠的评估器。同时，同一证据可能在不同候选中以对立立场出现，需要更精细的选择机制。

Method: 提出Best-of-Evidence (BoE)框架：保持BoN候选池固定，用带符号的候选-因子图表示可复用证据，将有限预算分配给能改变最终选择的证据行动。包含基于得分的实际控制器，零预算时退化为基础BoN决策。

Result: 理论证明：残余证据容量限制了任何证据驱动的改进；在因子-代码模型中，共享因子查询可实现O(log K)与Θ(K)的查询分离。在四个医疗VQA数据集上，BoE在证据可靠、对比性强且与决策相关时能改善固定池选择并挽救部分BoN失败，但也揭示了信道质量和候选生成对通用增益的限制。

Conclusion: BoE为部分验证条件下的推理时选择提供了形式化框架，在医疗领域展示了应用价值，但性能提升受限于证据质量、对比性及候选生成能力。

Abstract: BoN improves model outputs by sampling several candidates and selecting one with a proxy score, but it assumes that complete candidates can be evaluated reliably. Many vision-language tasks instead provide only partial verification: a finding, span, value, region, or relation may be checkable even when no dependable whole-response verifier exists. Moreover, the same claim may recur across candidates with opposing stances, allowing one observation to support part of the pool and contradict another. We introduce Best-of-Evidence (BoE), an inference-time selection framework that keeps the BoN candidate pool fixed, represents reusable claims with a signed candidate--factor graph, and allocates a limited budget to evidence actions that can change the final choice. BoE formalizes selection under partial verification and provides a practical score-based controller, with the zero-budget case recovering the underlying BoN decision. Theoretically, we show that residual evidence capacity limits any evidence-driven improvement and that shared factor queries can achieve an O(log K) versus Θ(K) query separation in a factor-code model. Common-ledger experiments on four medical VQA settings show that BoE can improve fixed-pool selection and rescue some BoN failures when evidence is reliable, contrastive, and decision-relevant, while also revealing the channel-quality and candidate-generation limits that prevent universal gains.

</details>


### [319] [The Weight of Silence: A Causal Case for Weights Over the Scratchpad in Latent Chess Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20952)
*Ishan S. Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过象棋任务检验了强化学习（RL）对潜在推理（latent reasoning）的影响。研究发现，RL训练后模型合法走子率从48%提升至61%，将杀局面幻觉完全消除。通过六组因果干预实验发现，即使替换或添加噪声到潜在思维向量，模型性能不变；仅精确零向量会造成崩溃，且RL后崩溃程度更轻（1% vs 9%）。这表明RL增加的是对干扰的鲁棒性，而非对思维内容的依赖，颠覆了"潜在思维作为推理时主动查阅的草稿板"的默认假设。潜在推理的主要作用是在训练阶段塑造模型参数。此外，该工作首次在象棋领域实现了RL增益，而此前多个团队在数学和逻辑任务中报告了相同配方无法超越SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果分析仅局限于数学和逻辑任务，且只比较单个检查点内模型对思维内容的依赖，从未在RL训练前后进行对比。本研究旨在检验RL是否改变了模型对潜在推理内部表征的依赖方式，验证"潜在思维作为推理时主动查阅的草稿板"这一默认假设是否成立。

Method: 训练一个象棋模型，采用分阶段潜在推理课程训练后接强化学习。设计六组因果干预实验（替换噪声、添加噪声、消融、精确零向量等），在同一模型RL训练前后分别测试，比较性能变化。

Result: RL后合法走子率从48%提升至61%，将杀幻觉完全消除。因果干预显示：替换或添加噪声不影响性能，消融仅轻微下降，仅精确零向量导致崩溃。RL后对精确零向量干扰的崩溃程度更轻（1%→9%），表明RL增加了鲁棒性而非依赖。

Conclusion: 潜在推理的主要作用是在训练阶段塑造模型参数，而非作为推理时主动查阅的草稿板。RL增加的是对干扰的鲁棒性，而非对思维内容的依赖。本研究还在象棋领域首次实现了RL增益，突破了此前数学/逻辑任务中RL未能超越SFT的困境。
